import React, { useRef, Suspense, useMemo, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Canvas, useFrame, useThree } from '@react-three/fiber';
import { OrbitControls, Html, ContactShadows, useGLTF } from '@react-three/drei';
import { SkeletonUtils } from 'three-stdlib';
import { User, GameState, AVATARS, getRankInfo, NAME_COLORS, TITLES } from './types';
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from "framer-motion";

const PLAYER_CENTER = [2, 0, -1]; 
const MAP_CONFIGS: { [key: string]: { position: [number, number, number], scale: number } } = {
    "floating_island__low_poly_vr.glb": { position: [0, -1.70, 0], scale: 0.5 },
    "snow.glb": { position: [2, -0.1, -1], scale: 1.5 }, 
    "desert.glb": { position: [4.0, 4.5, -1], scale: 7.5 }
};
const PLAYER_CIRCLE_RADIUS_SMALL = 3.5;
const PLAYER_CIRCLE_RADIUS_LARGE = 4.5;

function FirstPersonCamera({ myId, users, radius, onHeadRotation }: any) {
    const { camera, gl } = useThree();
    const lastEmitTime = useRef(0);
    const lastEmittedRot = useRef({ pitch: 0, yaw: 0 });
    const isDragging = useRef(false);
    const currentLook = useRef({ yaw: 0, pitch: 0 });
    const lastTargetLook = useRef({ yaw: 0, pitch: 0 });

    const lastPos = useRef({ x: 0, y: 0 });

    React.useEffect(() => {
        const handleDown = (e: PointerEvent) => { 
            if(e.isPrimary) {
                isDragging.current = true; 
                lastPos.current = { x: e.screenX, y: e.screenY };
                (e.target as HTMLElement).setPointerCapture?.(e.pointerId);
            }
        };
        const handleUp = (e: PointerEvent) => { 
            if(e.isPrimary) {
                isDragging.current = false; 
                (e.target as HTMLElement).releasePointerCapture?.(e.pointerId);
            }
        };
        const handleMove = (e: PointerEvent) => {
            if (isDragging.current && e.isPrimary) {
                const maxYaw = Math.PI / 2.5; 
                const maxPitch = Math.PI / 4; 
                
                const deltaX = e.screenX - lastPos.current.x;
                const deltaY = e.screenY - lastPos.current.y;
                lastPos.current = { x: e.screenX, y: e.screenY };

                const deltaYaw = -(deltaX / window.innerWidth) * Math.PI * 1.5;
                const deltaPitch = -(deltaY / window.innerHeight) * Math.PI * 1.5;
                
                lastTargetLook.current.yaw = Math.max(-maxYaw, Math.min(maxYaw, lastTargetLook.current.yaw + deltaYaw));
                lastTargetLook.current.pitch = Math.max(-maxPitch, Math.min(maxPitch, lastTargetLook.current.pitch + deltaPitch));
            }
        };

        const target = gl.domElement;
        target.addEventListener('pointerdown', handleDown as EventListener);
        target.addEventListener('pointerup', handleUp as EventListener);
        target.addEventListener('pointercancel', handleUp as EventListener);
        target.addEventListener('pointermove', handleMove as EventListener);
        return () => {
            target.removeEventListener('pointerdown', handleDown as EventListener);
            target.removeEventListener('pointerup', handleUp as EventListener);
            target.removeEventListener('pointercancel', handleUp as EventListener);
            target.removeEventListener('pointermove', handleMove as EventListener);
        }
    }, [gl.domElement]);

    useFrame((state, delta) => {
        const myIndex = users.findIndex((u: any) => u.id === myId);
        if (myIndex === -1) {
            camera.position.lerp(new THREE.Vector3(PLAYER_CENTER[0], 5, PLAYER_CENTER[2] + 8), 2 * delta);
            camera.lookAt(PLAYER_CENTER[0], 0, PLAYER_CENTER[2]);
            return;
        }

        const angleOffset = users.length === 3 ? (Math.PI / 2) : -(Math.PI / 2);
        const angle = (myIndex / users.length) * (2 * Math.PI) + angleOffset;
        
        const headX = PLAYER_CENTER[0] + Math.cos(angle) * radius;
        const headZ = PLAYER_CENTER[2] + Math.sin(angle) * radius;
        const headY = PLAYER_CENTER[1] + 1.2; 

        camera.position.lerp(new THREE.Vector3(headX, headY, headZ), 5 * delta);

        const targetX = PLAYER_CENTER[0];
        const targetY = PLAYER_CENTER[1] + 0.8;
        const targetZ = PLAYER_CENTER[2];

        camera.lookAt(targetX, targetY, targetZ);
        
        currentLook.current.yaw = THREE.MathUtils.lerp(currentLook.current.yaw, lastTargetLook.current.yaw, 10 * delta);
        currentLook.current.pitch = THREE.MathUtils.lerp(currentLook.current.pitch, lastTargetLook.current.pitch, 10 * delta);

        camera.rotateY(currentLook.current.yaw);
        camera.rotateX(currentLook.current.pitch);

        const pitch = currentLook.current.pitch;
        const yaw = currentLook.current.yaw;

        const now = Date.now();
        if (now - lastEmitTime.current > 100) {
            const pitchDeg = Math.round(pitch * 180 / Math.PI);
            const yawDeg = Math.round(yaw * 180 / Math.PI);
            if (lastEmittedRot.current.pitch !== pitchDeg || lastEmittedRot.current.yaw !== yawDeg) {
                lastEmittedRot.current = { pitch: pitchDeg, yaw: yawDeg };
                lastEmitTime.current = now;
                if (onHeadRotation) onHeadRotation(pitch, yaw);
            }
        }
    });

    return null;
}

function AvatarModel({ url, isWinner, targetRot }: { url: string, isWinner: boolean, targetRot?: { pitch: number, yaw: number } }) {
    const { scene } = useGLTF(`/avatars/${url}`);
    const groupRef = useRef<THREE.Group>(null);
    const headBone = useRef<THREE.Object3D | null>(null);

    const clonedScene = React.useMemo(() => {
        const clone = SkeletonUtils.clone(scene);
        clone.traverse((child: any) => {
            if (child.isMesh || child.isSkinnedMesh) {
                child.frustumCulled = false;
            }
        });
        return clone;
    }, [scene]);

    React.useEffect(() => {
        if (!groupRef.current) return;
        headBone.current = null;
        groupRef.current.traverse((child) => {
            if ((child as any).isBone) {
                const name = child.name.toLowerCase();
                if (name.includes('head') || name.includes('neck')) {
                    if (!headBone.current) headBone.current = child; 
                }
            }
        });
    }, [clonedScene]);

    useFrame((state, delta) => {
        if (targetRot) {
            if (headBone.current) {
                const bone = headBone.current as any;
                if (!bone.userData.initialRotation) {
                    bone.userData.initialRotation = bone.rotation.clone();
                }
                const initRot = bone.userData.initialRotation;
                bone.rotation.y = THREE.MathUtils.lerp(bone.rotation.y, initRot.y + targetRot.yaw, 5 * delta);
                bone.rotation.x = THREE.MathUtils.lerp(bone.rotation.x, initRot.x - targetRot.pitch, 5 * delta);
            } else if (groupRef.current) {
                groupRef.current.rotation.y = THREE.MathUtils.lerp(groupRef.current.rotation.y, targetRot.yaw, 5 * delta);
            }
        }
    });

    return (
        <group ref={groupRef} position={[0, 0.5, 0]} scale={0.6}>
            <primitive object={clonedScene} castShadow receiveShadow />
        </group>
    );
}

const sharedParticleSphereGeo = new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.5, 16, 16);
const sharedRingTorusGeo = new THREE.TorusGeometry(1, 0.1, 16, 32);
const sharedMirrorSphereGeo = new THREE.SphereGeometry(1.5, 32, 32);
const sharedFallbackCylinderGeo = new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.5, 0.5, 1, 32);
const sharedFallbackBoxGeo = new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.5, 1, 0.5);

const sharedGoldPedestalGeo = new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.8, 0.9, 0.5, 32);
const sharedLavaPedestalCylinderGeo = new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.7, 0.8, 0.4, 16);
const sharedLavaPedestalTorusGeo = new THREE.TorusGeometry(0.65, 0.12, 16, 32);
const sharedIcePedestalBoxGeo = new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.5, 0.5, 1.5);
const sharedCloudPedestalSphere1 = new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.6, 16, 16);
const sharedCloudPedestalSphere2 = new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.5, 16, 16);
const sharedCloudPedestalSphere3 = new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.45, 16, 16);
const sharedDefaultPedestalGeo = new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.7, 0.75, 0.5, 8);

function PedestalNode({ type }: { type: string }) {
    switch (type) {
        case 'gold_pedestal':
            return (
                <mesh position={[0, 0.25, 0]} castShadow receiveShadow geometry={sharedGoldPedestalGeo}>
                    <meshStandardMaterial color="#fbbf24" roughness={0.3} metalness={0.8} />
                </mesh>
            );
        case 'lava_ring':
            return (
                <group position={[0, 0.25, 0]}>
                    <mesh castShadow receiveShadow geometry={sharedLavaPedestalCylinderGeo}>
                        <meshStandardMaterial color="#1c1917" roughness={0.9} />
                    </mesh>
                    <mesh position={[0, 0.2, 0]} rotation={[Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]} geometry={sharedLavaPedestalTorusGeo}>
                        <meshStandardMaterial color="#ef4444" emissive="#dc2626" emissiveIntensity={2} toneMapped={false} />
                    </mesh>
                </group>
            );
        case 'ice_block':
            return (
                <mesh position={[0, 0.25, 0]} castShadow receiveShadow geometry={sharedIcePedestalBoxGeo}>
                    <meshPhysicalMaterial color="#bae6fd" transmission={0.9} opacity={1} transparent roughness={0.1} thickness={0.5} />
                </mesh>
            );
        case 'cloud_base':
            return (
                <group position={[0, 0.25, 0]}>
                    <mesh position={[0, 0, 0]} castShadow receiveShadow geometry={sharedCloudPedestalSphere1}><meshStandardMaterial color="#ffffff" roughness={1} /></mesh>
                    <mesh position={[0.4, -0.1, 0.3]} castShadow receiveShadow geometry={sharedCloudPedestalSphere2}><meshStandardMaterial color="#ffffff" roughness={1} /></mesh>
                    <mesh position={[-0.4, -0.1, 0.3]} castShadow receiveShadow geometry={sharedCloudPedestalSphere2}><meshStandardMaterial color="#ffffff" roughness={1} /></mesh>
                    <mesh position={[0.2, -0.1, -0.4]} castShadow receiveShadow geometry={sharedCloudPedestalSphere3}><meshStandardMaterial color="#ffffff" roughness={1} /></mesh>
                </group>
            );
        default:
            return (
                <mesh position={[0, 0.25, 0]} castShadow receiveShadow geometry={sharedDefaultPedestalGeo}>
                    <meshStandardMaterial color="#475569" roughness={0.9} metalness={0.1} />
                </mesh>
            );
    }
}

function ParticleBurst({ color }: { color: string }) {
    const meshRef = useRef<THREE.Mesh>(null);
    const materialRef = useRef<THREE.MeshStandardMaterial>(null);

    useFrame((state, delta) => {
        if (meshRef.current) {
            meshRef.current.scale.addScalar(delta * 20);
        }
        if (materialRef.current) {
            materialRef.current.opacity = Math.max(0, materialRef.current.opacity - delta * 2);
        }
    });

    useEffect(() => {
        return () => {
            materialRef.current?.dispose();
        };
    }, []);

    return (
        <mesh ref={meshRef} position={[0, 1.5, 0]} geometry={sharedParticleSphereGeo}>
            <meshStandardMaterial
                ref={materialRef}
                color={color}
                transparent
                opacity={0.8}
                emissive={color}
                emissiveIntensity={2}
            />
        </mesh>
    );
}

function RingBurst({ color }: { color: string }) {
    const meshRef = useRef<THREE.Mesh>(null);
    const materialRef = useRef<THREE.MeshStandardMaterial>(null);

    useFrame((state, delta) => {
        if (meshRef.current) {
            meshRef.current.scale.addScalar(delta * 10);
        }
        if (materialRef.current) {
            materialRef.current.opacity = Math.max(0, materialRef.current.opacity - delta * 1.5);
        }
    });

    useEffect(() => {
        return () => {
            materialRef.current?.dispose();
        };
    }, []);

    return (
        <mesh ref={meshRef} position={[0, 0.2, 0]} rotation={[-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]} geometry={sharedRingTorusGeo}>
            <meshStandardMaterial
                ref={materialRef}
                color={color}
                transparent
                opacity={1}
                emissive={color}
                emissiveIntensity={2}
            />
        </mesh>
    );
}

function PlayerNode({ u, index, totalUsers, isCurrentTurn, isMe, isWinner, activeBubble, gameState, headRotations, taunt, visualEffects }: any) {
    const radius = totalUsers > 4 ? PLAYER_CIRCLE_RADIUS_LARGE : PLAYER_CIRCLE_RADIUS_SMALL;
    const angleOffset = totalUsers === 3 ? (Math.PI / 2) : -(Math.PI / 2);
    const angle = (index / totalUsers) * (2 * Math.PI) + angleOffset;
    const x = Math.cos(angle) * radius;
    const z = Math.sin(angle) * radius;

    const userAvatar = AVATARS.find(a => a.id === u.avatar) || AVATARS[0];
    const groupRef = useRef<THREE.Group>(null);
    const bodyRef = useRef<THREE.Group>(null);

    const targetRot = headRotations?.[u.id];
    
    const bomb = u.timeBomb;
    const hasMirror = isMe && (u.mirrorRoundsLeft && u.mirrorRoundsLeft > 0);
    
    const myEffects = useMemo(() => {
        return visualEffects?.filter((e: any) => e.targetId === u.id || e.userId === u.id) || [];
    }, [visualEffects, u.id]);
    
    return (
        <group ref={groupRef} position={[x, 0, z]} rotation={[0, -angle - Math.PI / 2, 0]}>
            
            <PedestalNode type={u.pedestal || 'default_stone'} />

            {myEffects.map((eff: any) => {
                if (eff.type === 'bomb_explode') return <ParticleBurst key={eff.id} color="#ef4444" />;
                if (eff.type === 'roulette_die') return <ParticleBurst key={eff.id} color="#000000" />;
                if (eff.type === 'mind_wipe') return <ParticleBurst key={eff.id} color="#a855f7" />;
                if (eff.type === 'mirror_cast' || eff.type === 'shield_cast') return <ParticleBurst key={eff.id} color="#06b6d4" />;
                if (eff.type === 'time_bend') return <RingBurst key={eff.id} color="#eab308" />;
                if (eff.type === 'roulette_start') return <RingBurst key={eff.id} color="#dc2626" />;
                if (eff.type === 'blood_tie') return <RingBurst key={eff.id} color="#ef4444" />;
                if (eff.type === 'silence_cast') return <RingBurst key={eff.id} color="#71717a" />;
                if (eff.type === 'extra_ammo') return <ParticleBurst key={eff.id} color="#eab308" />;
                if (eff.type === 'letter_reveal') return <RingBurst key={eff.id} color="#22c55e" />;
                return null;
            })}

            {hasMirror && (
                <mesh position={[0, 1.2, 0]} geometry={sharedMirrorSphereGeo}>
                    <meshPhysicalMaterial color="#38bdf8" transmission={0.9} opacity={0.3} transparent roughness={0.1} />
                </mesh>
            )}

            {!isMe && (
                <group ref={bodyRef}>
                    {userAvatar.model || u.avatar.includes('default') ? (
                        <Suspense fallback={
                            <mesh position={[0, 1, 0]} geometry={sharedFallbackBoxGeo}><meshStandardMaterial color="gray"/></mesh>
                        }>
                            <AvatarModel url={userAvatar.model || "Warrior.gltf"} isWinner={isWinner} targetRot={targetRot} />
                        </Suspense>
                    ) : (
                        <mesh position={[0, 1, 0]} castShadow receiveShadow geometry={sharedFallbackCylinderGeo}>
                            <meshStandardMaterial color="gray" />
                        </mesh>
                    )}
                </group>
            )}
            
            {bomb && (
                <Html position={[0, 2.90, 0]} center zIndexRange={[95, 0]} style={{ pointerEvents: 'none' }}>
                    <div className="flex flex-col items-center animate-bounce">
                        <span className="text-4xl drop-shadow-[0_0_15px_rgba(239,68,68,0.8)]">💣</span>
                        <span className="text-[10px] font-black text-red-400 bg-black/80 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full mt-1 border border-red-500/50">
                            {bomb.roundsLeft} TUR
                        </span>
                    </div>
                </Html>
            )}

            {taunt && (
                <Html position={[0, 3.5, 0]} center zIndexRange={[95, 0]} style={{ pointerEvents: 'none' }}>
                    <AnimatePresence>
                        <motion.div
                            key={taunt.id}
                            initial={{ opacity: 1, y: 0, scale: 0.5 }}
                            animate={{ opacity: 0, y: -80, scale: 2 }}
                            transition={{ duration: 2, ease: "easeOut" }}
                            className="text-5xl drop-shadow-[0_10px_10px_rgba(0,0,0,0.8)] whitespace-nowrap"
                        >
                            {taunt.type === 'fire' && '🔥🔥🔥'}
                            {taunt.type === 'party' && '🎉✨🎉'}
                            {taunt.type === 'laugh' && '😂😂😂'}
                            {taunt.type === 'cry' && '😭😭💦'}
                        </motion.div>
                    </AnimatePresence>
                </Html>
            )}

            {!isMe && (
                <Html position={[0, 2.5, 0]} center zIndexRange={[100, 0]} style={{ pointerEvents: 'none' }}>
                    <div className='flex flex-col items-center select-none'>
                        {activeBubble && (
                            <div className='absolute -top-14 bg-white text-black px-3 py-1.5 rounded-2xl rounded-bl-none text-xs font-bold shadow-xl z-50 text-center whitespace-normal break-words' style={{ minWidth: 'max-content', maxWidth: '140px' }}>
                                {activeBubble}
                            </div>
                        )}
                        {!isWinner && (
                            <div className={`mb-1 px-3 py-1 rounded-lg border shadow-lg font-bold text-center text-xs max-w-[100px] break-words z-20 bg-yellow-100 border-yellow-400 text-black transform -rotate-2`}>
                                {u.assignedWord}
                            </div>
                        )}
                        <span className={`mt-1 font-bold text-[11px] bg-black/80 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full whitespace-nowrap shadow-lg flex items-center gap-1.5 ${u.status === 'spectator' ? 'line-through text-slate-500' : (u.nameColor ? (NAME_COLORS.find(c => c.id === u.nameColor)?.className || 'text-white') : 'text-white')} ${u.disconnected ? 'text-red-400' : ''}`}>
                            <span className={`flex items-center gap-1 ${getRankInfo(u.reputation || 0).color}`}>
                                <span className="text-[13px]">{getRankInfo(u.reputation || 0).icon}</span>
                                <span className="text-[9px] uppercase tracking-wider font-black">{getRankInfo(u.reputation || 0).title}</span>
                            </span>
                            {u.title && (
                                <span className="text-[8px] font-black px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-slate-700/50 text-white border border-slate-600">
                                    {TITLES.find(t => t.id === u.title)?.label || u.title}
                                </span>
                            )}
                            <span className="text-slate-500 mx-0.5">|</span>
                            {u.name} {u.disconnected && '(Koptu)'}
                        </span>
                        {u.status !== 'spectator' && !isWinner && (
                            <div className='flex gap-1 mt-1 bg-black/40 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full'>
                                {[...Array(3)].map((_, i) => (
                                    <span key={i} className='drop-shadow-md text-[8px]'>{i < (u.lives ?? 3) ? '❤️' : '🖤'}</span>
                                ))}
                            </div>
                        )}
                    </div>
                </Html>
            )}
        </group>
    );
}

function MapModel({ url }: { url: string }) {
    const { scene } = useGLTF(`/maps/${url}`);
    const config = MAP_CONFIGS[url] || { position: [0, 0, 0], scale: 1 };
    
    return (
        <group position={config.position} scale={config.scale}>
            <primitive object={scene} castShadow receiveShadow />
        </group>
    );
}

export default function Scene3D({ gameState, myId, activeBubbles, headRotations, onHeadRotation, taunts, visualEffects }: any) {
    const isDay = gameState?.theme !== "night";

    return (
        <div className='absolute inset-0 w-full h-full z-0 pointer-events-auto'>
            <Canvas shadows dpr={[1, 1.5]} camera={{ position: [0, 4.5, 8], fov: 45 }}>
                <color attach='background' args={[isDay ? '#38bdf8' : '#0f172a']} />
                <fog attach='fog' args={[isDay ? '#38bdf8' : '#0f172a', isDay ? 15 : 8, isDay ? 40 : 30]} />
                <ambientLight intensity={isDay ? 0.9 : 0.4} />
                <directionalLight castShadow position={[10, 15, 5]} intensity={isDay ? 2.2 : 1.2} color={isDay ? "#ffedd5" : "#e0f2fe"} shadow-mapSize={[1024, 1024]} />
                <pointLight position={[0, 4, 0]} intensity={isDay ? 0.5 : 1.5} color={isDay ? '#eab308' : '#38bdf8'} distance={10} />
                
                <Suspense fallback={
                    <mesh position={[0, -0.1, 0]} receiveShadow>
                        <cylinderGeometry args={[2.8, 3.2, 0.2, 64]} />
                        <meshStandardMaterial color='#334155' roughness={0.7} />
                    </mesh>
                }>
                <MapModel url={gameState?.map || "floating_island__low_poly_vr.glb"} />
            </Suspense>

            <group position={PLAYER_CENTER as [number, number, number]}>
                {gameState.users.map((u: any, i: number) => (
                    <PlayerNode 
                        key={u.id} 
                        u={u} 
                        index={i} 
                        totalUsers={gameState.users.length} 
                        isCurrentTurn={u.id === gameState.currentTurnUserId} 
                        isMe={u.id === myId} 
                        isWinner={gameState.winners.includes(u.id)} 
                        activeBubble={activeBubbles[u.id]?.text}
                        gameState={gameState}
                        headRotations={headRotations}
                        taunt={taunts?.[u.id]}
                        visualEffects={visualEffects}
                    />
                ))}
            </group>

            <ContactShadows position={[PLAYER_CENTER[0], PLAYER_CENTER[1] - 0.05, PLAYER_CENTER[2]]} opacity={0.5} scale={15} blur={2.5} far={4} />
            <FirstPersonCamera 
                myId={myId} 
                users={gameState.users} 
                radius={gameState.users.length > 4 ? PLAYER_CIRCLE_RADIUS_LARGE : PLAYER_CIRCLE_RADIUS_SMALL} 
                onHeadRotation={onHeadRotation}
            />
        </Canvas>
    </div>
);
}

DOSYA: client\components\game\WordSelection.tsx

import { Button, TextField, Chip } from "@mui/material";
import { User, GameState } from "./types";

interface WordSelectionProps {
    gameState: GameState;
    me: User;
    wordInput: string;
    setWordInput: (val: string) => void;
    onSetWord: () => void;
    onEditWord: () => void;
    onShuffleTargets: () => void;
    onLeaveRoom: () => void;
}

export default function WordSelection({
    gameState, me, wordInput, setWordInput, onSetWord, onEditWord, onShuffleTargets, onLeaveRoom
}: WordSelectionProps) {
    const targetUser = gameState.users.find((u: User) => u.id === me?.targetId);

    return (
        <main className="min-h-screen bg-slate-900 text-white flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-4 relative gap-12">
            <div className="glass max-w-lg w-full p-8 rounded-3xl text-center shadow-2xl relative overflow-hidden">
                {gameState.isBettingEnabled && (
                    <div className="absolute top-0 left-0 right-0 bg-yellow-500/20 border-b border-yellow-500/50 p-2 flex justify-center items-center gap-2">
                        <span className="text-xl">💰</span>
                        <span className="text-yellow-400 font-bold text-sm">BAHİS MODU AKTİF (-{gameState.betAmount || 50} Altın)</span>
                    </div>
                )}
                <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-3 mb-2">
                    <h2 className={`text-3xl font-bold text-cyan-400 ${gameState.isBettingEnabled ? 'mt-6' : ''}`}>Kelime Seçimi</h2>
                    {me?.isHost && (
                        <Button variant="outlined" color="info" size="small" onClick={onShuffleTargets} className={`rounded-full min-w-0 w-10 h-10 p-0 ${gameState.isBettingEnabled ? 'mt-6' : ''}`} title="Hedefleri Karıştır">
                            🎲
                        </Button>
                    )}
                </div>
                {gameState.category && (
                    <Chip label={`Kategori: ${gameState.category}`} color="secondary" className="mb-6 font-bold" />
                )}
                {me?.hasSubmittedWord ? (
                    <div className="py-8">
                        <p className="text-xl mb-2">Kelimeyi gönderdin!</p>
                        <p className="text-xl font-black text-fuchsia-400 mb-6 uppercase tracking-widest">{targetUser?.assignedWord}</p>
                        <Button variant="outlined" color="warning" onClick={onEditWord} className="mb-6 font-bold border-2">
                            Kelimeyi Düzenle ✏️
                        </Button>
                        <p className="text-slate-400 animate-pulse">Diğer oyuncuların kelimelerini seçmesi bekleniyor...</p>
                    </div>
                ) : (
                    <div className="space-y-6 text-left">
                        <div className="bg-slate-800 p-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-700">
                            <p className="text-slate-400 text-sm mb-1">Şu oyuncu için bir kelime/isim belirle:</p>
                            <p className="text-2xl font-bold text-white">{targetUser?.name}</p>
                        </div>
                        <TextField 
                            fullWidth
                            label="Kafasında ne yazsın?"
                            variant="outlined"
                            value={wordInput}
                            onChange={(e) => setWordInput(e.target.value)}
                            sx={{
                                input: { color: 'white' },
                                label: { color: '#94a3b8' },
                                '& .MuiOutlinedInput-root': {
                                    '& fieldset': { borderColor: '#475569' },
                                    '&:hover fieldset': { borderColor: '#94a3b8' },
                                    '&.Mui-focused fieldset': { borderColor: '#a855f7' }
                                }
                            }}
                        />
                        <Button variant="contained" color="primary" fullWidth size="large" onClick={onSetWord} className="py-3">
                            Onayla
                        </Button>
                    </div>
                )}

                <div className="mt-8 pt-4 border-t border-slate-700 text-left">
                    <p className="text-sm text-slate-400 mb-2">Durum:</p>
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                        {gameState.users.map((u: User) => (
                            <Chip key={u.id} label={u.name} color={u.hasSubmittedWord ? "success" : "default"} variant="outlined" />
                        ))}
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
            
            <Button variant="outlined" color="inherit" size="small" onClick={onLeaveRoom} className="border-slate-700 text-slate-400 hover:text-red-400 hover:border-red-500 hover:bg-red-500/10">
                Odadan Ayrıl
            </Button>
        </main>
    );
}

    jokerDraftEndTime?: number;
    roundCount?: number;

        initiator: string;

        question: string;
        votes: { [userId: string]: string };
        endTime?: number;
    } | null;
    resolvedQuestionsThisTurn?: { askerId: string; question: string; votes: { [userId: string]: string }; }[];
    users: User[];
    currentTurnUserId: string | null;
    turnEndsAt: number | null;
    chatHistory?: any[];
    winners: string[];
    hasObjectionUsed?: boolean;
};

export const getRankInfo = (reputation: number) => {
    let title = "Çaylak";
    let color = "text-amber-600";
    let icon = <GiFootprint />;
    if (reputation >= 5000) { title = "Kimbuki Üstadı"; color = "text-yellow-400 font-black drop-shadow-[0_0_8px_rgba(251,191,36,0.8)]"; icon = <GiCrown />; }
    else if (reputation >= 2500) { title = "Zihin Okuyucu"; color = "text-cyan-300"; icon = <GiCrystalBall />; }
    else if (reputation >= 1000) { title = "Dedektif"; color = "text-yellow-500"; icon = <GiMagnifyingGlass />; }
    else if (reputation >= 400) { title = "Gözlemci"; color = "text-slate-300"; icon = <GiEyeTarget />; }
    return { title, color, icon };
};

DOSYA: client\components\game\ui\ActionMenu.tsx

import React from "react";
import { Button, CircularProgress } from "@mui/material";
import QuestionAnswerIcon from '@mui/icons-material/QuestionAnswer';
import SkipNextIcon from '@mui/icons-material/SkipNext';
import { motion } from "framer-motion";
import { useGameContext } from "@/context/GameContext";

interface ActionMenuProps {
    isActionsOpen: boolean;
    onToggle: () => void;
    onOpenQuestionDialog: () => void;
    onOpenGuessDialog: () => void;
    onOpenJokerDialog: () => void;
    isMyTurn?: boolean;
    currentRound?: number;
    isRussianRoulette?: boolean;
    hasActiveQuestion?: boolean;
    questionsAskedThisTurn?: number;
    extraQuestions?: number;
    hasJoker?: boolean;
    hasUsedJoker?: boolean;
    hasObjectionUsed?: boolean;
    onSkip?: () => void;
    onStartObjection?: () => void;
}

export default function ActionMenu(props: ActionMenuProps) {
    const ctx = useGameContext();

    const isMyTurn = props.isMyTurn ?? ctx.isMyTurn;
    const currentRound = props.currentRound ?? (ctx.gameState?.roundCount || 1);
    const isRussianRoulette = props.isRussianRoulette ?? !!ctx.me?.russianRouletteActive;
    const hasActiveQuestion = props.hasActiveQuestion ?? !!ctx.gameState?.activeQuestion;
    const questionsAskedThisTurn = props.questionsAskedThisTurn ?? (ctx.me?.questionsAskedThisTurn ?? 0);
    const extraQuestions = props.extraQuestions ?? (ctx.me?.extraQuestions ?? 0);
    const hasJoker = props.hasJoker ?? (ctx.me?.joker !== undefined && ctx.me?.joker !== null);
    const hasUsedJoker = props.hasUsedJoker ?? !!ctx.me?.hasUsedJoker;
    const hasObjectionUsed = props.hasObjectionUsed ?? !!ctx.gameState?.hasObjectionUsed;
    const onSkip = props.onSkip ?? ctx.actions.skip;
    const onStartObjection = props.onStartObjection ?? ctx.actions.startObjection;
    const isSubmitting = ctx.isSubmitting;

    const {
        isActionsOpen,
        onToggle,
        onOpenQuestionDialog,
        onOpenGuessDialog,
        onOpenJokerDialog
    } = props;

    return (
        <div className="order-1 md:order-3 w-full md:w-1/4 md:max-w-[400px] flex flex-col gap-3 pointer-events-auto z-40">
            <div className="glass rounded-2xl flex flex-col border border-slate-700/50 shadow-xl bg-slate-900/60 backdrop-blur-lg overflow-hidden transition-all duration-300">
                <div onClick={onToggle} className="p-3 md:hidden flex justify-between items-center cursor-pointer border-b border-slate-700/50 bg-slate-800/80">
                    <span className="font-bold text-sm text-green-400 flex items-center gap-2">⚡ Aksiyonlar</span>
                    <span className="text-xs bg-slate-700 px-2 py-1 rounded-lg text-slate-300">{isActionsOpen ? '▼ Gizle' : '▲ Aç'}</span>
                </div>

                <div className={`p-4 flex-col justify-center items-center gap-3 ${isActionsOpen ? 'flex' : 'hidden md:flex'}`}>
                    <motion.div whileHover={{ scale: isMyTurn && !isSubmitting ? 1.05 : 1 }} whileTap={{ scale: isMyTurn && !isSubmitting ? 0.95 : 1 }} className="w-full">
                        <Button 
                            variant="contained" 
                            color="info" 
                            size="medium" 
                            fullWidth 
                            startIcon={isSubmitting ? <CircularProgress size={18} color="inherit" /> : <QuestionAnswerIcon />} 
                            onClick={onOpenQuestionDialog} 
                            disabled={!isMyTurn || (questionsAskedThisTurn >= 1 && extraQuestions <= 0) || hasActiveQuestion || isRussianRoulette || isSubmitting} 
                            className={`py-3 rounded-xl border-2 font-bold ${isMyTurn && !hasActiveQuestion && !isRussianRoulette && !isSubmitting ? 'bg-cyan-600 shadow-lg shadow-cyan-500/30' : 'opacity-50'}`}
                        >
                            Soru Sor & Oylat
                        </Button>
                    </motion.div>

                    <div className="w-full h-px bg-slate-700 my-2"></div>

                    <motion.div whileHover={{ scale: isMyTurn && !isSubmitting ? 1.05 : 1 }} whileTap={{ scale: isMyTurn && !isSubmitting ? 0.95 : 1 }} className="w-full">
                        <Button 
                            variant="contained" 
                            color="success" 
                            size="medium" 
                            fullWidth 
                            startIcon={isSubmitting ? <CircularProgress size={18} color="inherit" /> : undefined}
                            onClick={onOpenGuessDialog} 
                            disabled={!isMyTurn || isSubmitting} 
                            className={`py-3 rounded-xl text-lg font-bold shadow-lg shadow-green-500/20 ${!isMyTurn || isSubmitting ? 'opacity-50' : ''}`}
                        >
                            TAHMİN ET
                        </Button>
                    </motion.div>

                    <motion.div whileHover={{ scale: isMyTurn && !isSubmitting ? 1.05 : 1 }} whileTap={{ scale: isMyTurn && !isSubmitting ? 0.95 : 1 }} className="w-full">
                        <Button 
                            variant="outlined" 
                            color="warning" 
                            size="medium" 
                            fullWidth 
                            startIcon={isSubmitting ? <CircularProgress size={18} color="inherit" /> : <SkipNextIcon />} 
                            onClick={onSkip} 
                            disabled={!isMyTurn || isRussianRoulette || isSubmitting} 
                            className={`py-3 rounded-xl border-2 ${isMyTurn && !isRussianRoulette && !isSubmitting ? 'hover:bg-warning-main/10' : 'opacity-50'}`}
                        >
                            Turu Geç
                        </Button>
                    </motion.div>

                    {hasJoker && !hasUsedJoker && (
                        <motion.div whileHover={{ scale: isMyTurn && !isSubmitting ? 1.05 : 1 }} whileTap={{ scale: isMyTurn && !isSubmitting ? 0.95 : 1 }} className="w-full mt-2">
                            <Button 
                                variant="contained" 
                                color="secondary" 
                                size="medium" 
                                fullWidth 
                                startIcon={isSubmitting ? <CircularProgress size={18} color="inherit" /> : undefined}
                                onClick={onOpenJokerDialog} 
                                disabled={currentRound < 3 || isRussianRoulette || !isMyTurn || isSubmitting} 
                                className={`py-3 rounded-xl border-2 font-bold ${currentRound >= 3 && !isRussianRoulette && isMyTurn && !isSubmitting ? 'bg-fuchsia-600 shadow-[0_0_15px_rgba(192,38,211,0.5)]' : 'opacity-50'}`}
                            >
                                {currentRound < 3 ? '🃏 Joker (3. Turda Açılır)' : '🃏 Joker Kullan'}
                            </Button>
                        </motion.div>
                    )}

                    <motion.div whileHover={{ scale: !hasObjectionUsed && !isSubmitting ? 1.05 : 1 }} whileTap={{ scale: !hasObjectionUsed && !isSubmitting ? 0.95 : 1 }} className="w-full mt-4">
                        <Button 
                            variant="outlined" 
                            color="error" 
                            size="medium" 
                            fullWidth 
                            startIcon={isSubmitting ? <CircularProgress size={18} color="inherit" /> : undefined}
                            onClick={onStartObjection} 
                            disabled={hasObjectionUsed || isRussianRoulette || isSubmitting} 
                            className={`py-2 rounded-xl border-2 font-bold ${!hasObjectionUsed && !isRussianRoulette && !isSubmitting ? 'border-red-500/80 text-red-400 shadow-[0_0_15px_rgba(239,68,68,0.2)] hover:bg-red-500/20' : 'opacity-50'}`}
                        >
                            🚨 ŞİKEYE İTİRAZ ET {hasObjectionUsed && '(Kullanıldı)'}
                        </Button>
                    </motion.div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
}


=========================
DOSYA: client\components\game\ui\ChatPanel.tsx
=========================

import React, { useRef, useEffect } from "react";
import { TextField, IconButton } from "@mui/material";
import SendIcon from '@mui/icons-material/Send';
import { NAME_COLORS } from "../types";
import { useGameChat } from "@/context/GameContext";

interface ChatPanelProps {
    chatHistory?: any[];
    chatInput?: string;
    setChatInput?: (val: string) => void;
    handleChat?: (e?: React.FormEvent) => void;
    isChatOpen: boolean;
    onToggle: () => void;
    isPlayer?: boolean;
}

export default function ChatPanel(props: ChatPanelProps) {
    const ctx = useGameChat();
    const chatHistory = props.chatHistory ?? ctx.chatHistory;
    const chatInput = props.chatInput ?? ctx.chatInput;
    const setChatInput = props.setChatInput ?? ctx.setChatInput;
    const handleChat = props.handleChat ?? ctx.handleChat;
    const isPlayer = props.isPlayer ?? ctx.isPlayer;
    const { isChatOpen, onToggle } = props;

    const chatEndRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

    useEffect(() => {
        chatEndRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth" });
    }, [chatHistory]);

    return (
        <div className="bg-slate-900/80 backdrop-blur-md border border-slate-700/50 rounded-2xl transition-all duration-300 flex flex-col z-10 relative pointer-events-auto shadow-2xl">
            <div onClick={onToggle} className="p-3 flex justify-between items-center cursor-pointer border-b border-slate-700/50">
                <span className="font-bold text-sm text-violet-400 flex items-center gap-2">💬 Sohbet</span>
                <span className="text-xs bg-slate-800 px-2 py-1 rounded-lg text-slate-300">{isChatOpen ? '▼ Gizle' : '▲ Aç'}</span>
            </div>

            <div className={`group ${isChatOpen ? 'flex flex-col' : 'hidden'}`}>
                <div className="h-40 group-focus-within:h-64 md:group-focus-within:h-72 transition-all duration-300 ease-in-out overflow-y-auto p-4 space-y-2 custom-scrollbar pointer-events-auto">
                    {chatHistory.map((msg: any, i: number) => {
                        let colorClass = "text-violet-400";
                        if (!msg.system && msg.nameColor) {
                            const found = NAME_COLORS.find(c => c.id === msg.nameColor);
                            if (found) colorClass = found.className;
                        }
                        return (
                            <div key={i} className={`text-sm ${msg.system ? (msg.message.includes('Sistem (Özel)') ? 'text-fuchsia-400 font-bold' : 'text-cyan-400 text-center italic my-2') : ''}`}>
                                {!msg.system && <strong className={colorClass}>{msg.name}: </strong>}
                                <span className={`break-words break-all ${msg.system ? (msg.message.includes('Sistem (Özel)') ? '' : 'font-bold') : 'text-slate-200'}`}>{msg.message}</span>
                            </div>
                        );
                    })}
                    <div ref={chatEndRef} />
                </div>
                <form onSubmit={handleChat} className="p-2 border-t border-slate-700 flex gap-2 pointer-events-auto">
                    <TextField 
                        fullWidth 
                        size="small" 
                        slotProps={{ htmlInput: { maxLength: 120 } }} 
                        placeholder={isPlayer ? "Soru sor veya cevapla..." : "İzleyici sohbeti..."} 
                        value={chatInput} 
                        onChange={e => setChatInput(e.target.value)} 
                        sx={{ 
                            input: { color: 'white' }, 
                            '& .MuiOutlinedInput-root': { 
                                '& fieldset': { borderColor: '#475569' }, 
                                '&:hover fieldset': { borderColor: '#94a3b8' }, 
                                '&.Mui-focused fieldset': { borderColor: '#06b6d4' } 
                            } 
                        }} 
                    />
                    <IconButton type="submit" color="primary" className="bg-violet-600 hover:bg-violet-500 text-white rounded-lg px-4">
                        <SendIcon />
                    </IconButton>
                </form>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
}

DOSYA: client\components\game\ui\ConnectionStatus.tsx

"use client";

import React from "react";
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from "framer-motion";
import WifiIcon from "@mui/icons-material/Wifi";
import WifiOffIcon from "@mui/icons-material/WifiOff";

interface ConnectionStatusProps {
    isConnected: boolean;
    isReconnecting: boolean;
    pingMs: number | null;
    connectionError?: string | null;
}

export default function ConnectionStatus({
    isConnected,
    isReconnecting,
    pingMs,
    connectionError,
}: ConnectionStatusProps) {
    let pingColor = "text-emerald-400 border-emerald-500/30 bg-emerald-950/40";
    let pingDot = "bg-emerald-400";
    if (pingMs !== null) {
        if (pingMs > 180) {
            pingColor = "text-rose-400 border-rose-500/30 bg-rose-950/40";
            pingDot = "bg-rose-400";
        } else if (pingMs > 90) {
            pingColor = "text-amber-400 border-amber-500/30 bg-amber-950/40";
            pingDot = "bg-amber-400";
        }
    }

    return (
        <>
            <AnimatePresence>
                {isReconnecting && (
                    <motion.div
                        initial={{ opacity: 0, y: -40 }}
                        animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                        exit={{ opacity: 0, y: -40 }}
                        transition={{ duration: 0.3 }}
                        className="fixed top-4 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 z-50 pointer-events-auto"
                    >
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 px-5 py-2.5 rounded-full bg-rose-600/95 text-white shadow-2xl backdrop-blur-md border border-rose-400/50 animate-pulse text-sm font-semibold">
                            <WifiOffIcon className="w-5 h-5 animate-spin" />
                            <span>Bağlantı kesildi, sunucuya yeniden bağlanılıyor...</span>
                        </div>
                    </motion.div>
                )}
            </AnimatePresence>

            <div className="fixed top-3 right-3 z-40 flex items-center gap-2 pointer-events-auto select-none">
                <div
                    className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-mono font-medium border backdrop-blur-md transition-all shadow-md ${
                        isReconnecting
                            ? "text-rose-300 border-rose-500/50 bg-rose-950/60 animate-pulse"
                            : pingColor
                    }`}
                    title={
                        isReconnecting
                            ? connectionError || "Yeniden bağlanılıyor..."
                            : `Ağ Gecikmesi: ${pingMs !== null ? pingMs + "ms" : "Hesaplanıyor..."}`
                    }
                >
                    <span
                        className={`w-2 h-2 rounded-full ${
                            isReconnecting ? "bg-rose-500 animate-ping" : pingDot
                        }`}
                    />
                    {isReconnecting ? (
                        <span>Yeniden Bağlanıyor</span>
                    ) : (
                        <span>{pingMs !== null ? `${pingMs}ms` : "Bağlı"}</span>
                    )}
                </div>
            </div>
        </>
    );
}


=========================
DOSYA: client\components\game\ui\Notepad.tsx
=========================

import React from "react";
import { Chip } from "@mui/material";
import { User, GameState, JOKERS } from "../types";
import { useGameNotepad } from "@/context/GameContext";

interface NotepadProps {
    notepad?: string;
    setNotepad?: (val: string) => void;
    isNotepadOpen: boolean;
    onToggle: () => void;
    me?: User;
    gameState?: GameState;
}

export default function Notepad(props: NotepadProps) {
    const ctx = useGameNotepad();
    const notepad = props.notepad ?? ctx.notepad;
    const setNotepad = props.setNotepad ?? ctx.setNotepad;
    const me = props.me ?? ctx.me;
    const gameState = props.gameState ?? ctx.gameState;
    const { isNotepadOpen, onToggle } = props;

    const myJokerMeta = JOKERS.find(j => j.id === me?.joker);

    return (
        <div className="order-2 md:order-1 w-full md:w-1/4 md:max-w-[400px] md:self-start glass p-3 rounded-2xl flex flex-col border border-slate-700/50 pointer-events-auto shadow-2xl bg-slate-900/60 backdrop-blur-lg transition-all duration-300 h-auto max-h-[85vh] overflow-y-auto custom-scrollbar">
            <h3 onClick={onToggle} className="font-bold text-lg flex items-center justify-between gap-2 text-yellow-400 cursor-pointer select-none hover:text-yellow-300">
                <span className="flex items-center gap-2">📝 Not Defterim</span>
                <span className="text-sm bg-slate-800/80 px-2 py-1 rounded-lg">{isNotepadOpen ? '▲ Gizle' : '▼ Aç'}</span>
            </h3>
            {isNotepadOpen && (
                <textarea
                    placeholder="Örn: Gerçek bir insan mı? Yaşıyor mu?..."
                    value={notepad}
                    onChange={e => setNotepad(e.target.value)}
                    spellCheck="false"
                    className="w-full bg-yellow-900/20 rounded-xl text-slate-200 p-3 mt-2 border border-yellow-500/30 focus:border-yellow-500/60 outline-none resize-none h-[150px] md:h-[250px] custom-scrollbar shrink-0"
                />
            )}

            {myJokerMeta && (
                <div className={`mt-4 p-3 rounded-xl border ${me.hasUsedJoker ? 'bg-slate-800/50 border-slate-700/50 opacity-60' : (myJokerMeta.rarity === 'prismatic' ? 'bg-fuchsia-900/30 border-fuchsia-500/30' : myJokerMeta.rarity === 'gold' ? 'bg-amber-900/30 border-amber-500/30' : 'bg-slate-700/30 border-slate-400/30')}`}>
                    <h4 className={`font-bold text-sm flex items-center gap-1 mb-1 ${me.hasUsedJoker ? 'text-slate-400' : (myJokerMeta.rarity === 'prismatic' ? 'text-fuchsia-400' : myJokerMeta.rarity === 'gold' ? 'text-amber-400' : 'text-slate-200')}`}>
                        🃏 {myJokerMeta.name} {me.hasUsedJoker && <span className="text-red-400 text-xs ml-auto">(Kullanıldı)</span>}
                    </h4>
                    <p className="text-xs text-slate-300">{myJokerMeta.desc}</p>

                    {(me.shieldActive || (me.mirrorRoundsLeft ?? 0) > 0) && (
                        <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-1.5 pt-2 border-t border-slate-700/50">
                            {me.shieldActive && (
                                <Chip size="small" label="🛡️ Gizli Kalkan Aktif" color="info" className="font-bold text-xs" />
                            )}
                            {(me.mirrorRoundsLeft ?? 0) > 0 ? (
                                <Chip size="small" label={`🪞 Ayna (${me.mirrorRoundsLeft} Tur)`} color="secondary" className="font-bold text-xs" />
                            ) : null}
                        </div>
                    )}
                </div>
            )}

            {me?.lastQuestions && me.lastQuestions.length > 0 && (
                <div className="mt-4 flex flex-col gap-3 overflow-y-auto custom-scrollbar max-h-[300px] pr-2">
                    {me.lastQuestions.map((q: any, i: number) => (
                        <div key={i} className="p-3 rounded-xl border bg-cyan-900/30 border-cyan-500/30">
                            <h4 className="font-bold text-sm text-cyan-400 mb-1">{me.lastQuestions!.length > 1 ? `${i + 1}. Sorum` : 'Son Sorduğum Soru'}</h4>
                            <p className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-200 mb-2 break-words break-all">"{q.question}"</p>
                            <div className="flex gap-2 text-xs flex-wrap">
                                {Object.entries(q.votes).map(([voterId, vote]) => {
                                    const voter = gameState.users.find((u: any) => u.id === voterId);
                                    if (!voter) return null;
                                    const voteColor = vote === 'yes' ? 'text-green-400' : vote === 'no' ? 'text-red-400' : 'text-yellow-400';
                                    const voteText = vote === 'yes' ? 'Evet' : vote === 'no' ? 'Hayır' : 'Bazen';
                                    return (
                                        <div key={voterId} className="bg-slate-800/80 px-2 py-1 rounded border border-slate-700">
                                            <span className="text-slate-400 mr-1">{voter.name}:</span>
                                            <span className={`font-bold ${voteColor}`}>{voteText}</span>
                                        </div>
                                    );
                                })}
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    ))}
                </div>
            )}
        </div>
    );
}


=========================
DOSYA: client\context\GameContext.tsx
=========================

"use client";

import React, { createContext, useContext, ReactNode } from "react";
import { GameState, User } from "@/components/game/types";
import { GameActions } from "@/hooks/useGameSocket";

export interface GameContextValue {
    // Temel Oyun ve Oyuncu Durumu
    gameState: GameState;
    me: User;
    myId: string;
    isMyTurn: boolean;
    currentTurnUser?: User;

   
    actions: GameActions;

   
    notepad: string;
    setNotepad: (val: string) => void;

    
    chatInput: string;
    setChatInput: (val: string) => void;

   
    guessInput: string;
    setGuessInput: (val: string) => void;
    guessDialogOpen: boolean;
    setGuessDialogOpen: (val: boolean) => void;

    headRotations: { [key: string]: { pitch: number; yaw: number } };
    taunts: { [key: string]: { type: string; id: number } };
    visualEffects: any[];

    isSubmitting: boolean;
    isConnected: boolean;
    isReconnecting: boolean;
    pingMs: number | null;
    connectionError: string | null;
}

const GameContext = createContext<GameContextValue | null>(null);

export function GameProvider({
    value,
    children,
}: {
    value: GameContextValue;
    children: ReactNode;
}) {
    return <GameContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</GameContext.Provider>;
}

export function useGameContext(): GameContextValue {
    const context = useContext(GameContext);
    if (!context) {
        throw new Error("useGameContext must be used within a GameProvider");
    }
    return context;
}

export function useGameActions(): GameActions {
    const { actions } = useGameContext();
    return actions;
}

export function useGameTurn() {
    const { isMyTurn, currentTurnUser, gameState } = useGameContext();
    return {
        isMyTurn,
        currentTurnUser,
        currentTurnUserId: gameState.currentTurnUserId,
        turnEndsAt: gameState.turnEndsAt,
    };
}

export function useGameNotepad() {
    const { notepad, setNotepad, me, gameState } = useGameContext();
    return { notepad, setNotepad, me, gameState };
}

export function useGameChat() {
    const { gameState, chatInput, setChatInput, actions, me } = useGameContext();
    return {
        chatHistory: gameState.chatHistory || [],
        chatInput,
        setChatInput,
        handleChat: actions.chat,
        isPlayer: me?.status === "playing",
    };
}


=========================
DOSYA: client\hooks\useGameSocket.tsx
=========================

"use client";

import React, { useEffect, useState, useCallback, useRef, useMemo } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { io, Socket } from "socket.io-client";
import { toast } from "react-toastify";
import { Button } from "@mui/material";
import { User, GameState } from "@/components/game/types";

export interface UseGameSocketOptions {
    onClearNotepad?: () => void;
}

export interface GameStartSettings {
    category?: string;
    bettingEnabled?: boolean;
    jokersEnabled?: boolean;
    betAmount?: number;
    theme?: "day" | "night";
    map?: string;
}

export interface GameActions {
    start: (settings: GameStartSettings) => void;
    setWord: (customWord?: string) => void;
    chat: (e?: React.FormEvent, customMsg?: string) => void;
    skip: () => void;
    askQuestion: (question: string) => void;
    submitVote: (vote: string) => void;
    guess: (customGuess?: string) => void;
    useJoker: (payload: any) => void;
    toggleVoice: (enabled: boolean) => Promise<void>;
    copyLink: () => void;
    leaveRoom: () => void;
    selectAvatar: (id: number) => void;
    placeBet: (targetId: string) => void;
    editWord: () => void;
    shuffleTargets: () => void;
    selectJoker: (jokerId: number) => void;
    startObjection: () => void;
    voteObjection: (vote: boolean) => void;
    headRotation: (pitch: number, yaw: number) => void;
    taunt: (type: string) => void;
    closeRoom: () => void;
}

export function useGameSocket(
    code: string | string[] | undefined,
    options?: UseGameSocketOptions
) {
    const router = useRouter();
    const [socket, setSocket] = useState<Socket | null>(null);
    const [gameState, setGameState] = useState<GameState | null>(null);
    const [inviteUrl, setInviteUrl] = useState<string>("");

    const [isJoined, setIsJoined] = useState(false);
    const [checkingAuth, setCheckingAuth] = useState(true);

    const [isConnected, setIsConnected] = useState(false);
    const [isReconnecting, setIsReconnecting] = useState(false);
    const [pingMs, setPingMs] = useState<number | null>(null);
    const [connectionError, setConnectionError] = useState<string | null>(null);

    const [isSubmitting, setIsSubmitting] = useState(false);
    const submittingTimeoutRef = useRef<NodeJS.Timeout | null>(null);

    const markSubmitting = useCallback(() => {
        setIsSubmitting(true);
        if (submittingTimeoutRef.current) clearTimeout(submittingTimeoutRef.current);
        submittingTimeoutRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
            setIsSubmitting(false);
        }, 6000);
    }, []);

    const clearSubmitting = useCallback(() => {
        setIsSubmitting(false);
        if (submittingTimeoutRef.current) {
            clearTimeout(submittingTimeoutRef.current);
            submittingTimeoutRef.current = null;
        }
    }, []);

    useEffect(() => {
        return () => {
            if (submittingTimeoutRef.current) clearTimeout(submittingTimeoutRef.current);
        };
    }, []);

    const [wordInput, setWordInput] = useState("");
    const [chatInput, setChatInput] = useState("");
    const [guessDialogOpen, setGuessDialogOpen] = useState(false);
    const [guessInput, setGuessInput] = useState("");

    const [headRotations, setHeadRotations] = useState<{ [key: string]: { pitch: number; yaw: number } }>({});
    const [taunts, setTaunts] = useState<{ [key: string]: { type: string; id: number } }>({});
    const [visualEffects, setVisualEffects] = useState<any[]>([]);

    const optionsRef = useRef(options);
    optionsRef.current = options;

    const userRef = useRef<any>(null);

    const wordInputRef = useRef(wordInput);
    wordInputRef.current = wordInput;

    const chatInputRef = useRef(chatInput);
    chatInputRef.current = chatInput;

    const guessInputRef = useRef(guessInput);
    guessInputRef.current = guessInput;

    const myId = socket?.id || "";
    const me = gameState?.users.find((u: User) => u.id === myId);

    
    useEffect(() => {
        if (code && typeof window !== "undefined") {
            const roomCode = Array.isArray(code) ? code[0] : code;
            setInviteUrl(`${window.location.origin}/game/${roomCode}`);
        }
    }, [code]);

    const connectToRoom = useCallback((user: any) => {
        userRef.current = user;
        const token = localStorage.getItem("gameToken");
        const s = io(process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SERVER_URL || "http://localhost:4000", {
            auth: { token },
            reconnection: true,
            reconnectionAttempts: Infinity,
            reconnectionDelay: 1000,
            reconnectionDelayMax: 5000,
            timeout: 10000,
        });
        setSocket(s);

        const roomCode = Array.isArray(code) ? code[0] : code;

        const emitJoin = () => {
            if (!userRef.current) return;
            s.emit(
                "room:join",
                {
                    roomCode,
                    name: userRef.current.username,
                    avatar: userRef.current.avatar,
                    pedestal: userRef.current.pedestal,
                    nameColor: userRef.current.nameColor,
                    title: userRef.current.title,
                },
                (res: any) => {
                    if (!res.ok) {
                        toast.error("Hata: " + res.error);
                        router.push("/");
                    } else {
                        setIsJoined(true);
                        setCheckingAuth(false);
                    }
                }
            );
        };

        s.on("connect", () => {
            setIsConnected(true);
            setIsReconnecting(false);
            setConnectionError(null);
            emitJoin();
        });

        s.on("disconnect", (reason) => {
            setIsConnected(false);
            if (reason !== "io client disconnect") {
                setIsReconnecting(true);
            }
        });

        s.on("connect_error", (err) => {
            setIsConnected(false);
            setIsReconnecting(true);
            setConnectionError(err?.message || "Sunucuya bağlanılamıyor...");
        });

        s.on("game:reconnected", (data) => {
            toast.success(data?.message || "Odaya yeniden bağlandınız!", { toastId: "reconnect-success" });
        });

        s.on("game:pong", ({ clientTimestamp }: { clientTimestamp: number }) => {
            if (clientTimestamp) {
                setPingMs(Date.now() - clientTimestamp);
            }
        });

        s.on("game:state", (state: GameState) => {
            clearSubmitting();
            setGuessDialogOpen(false);
            setGameState((prev) => ({
                ...state,
                chatHistory: prev?.chatHistory || state.chatHistory || [],
            }));
        });

        s.on("game:init_chat", ({ chatHistory }: { chatHistory: any[] }) => {
            setGameState((prev) => {
                if (!prev) return prev;
                return {
                    ...prev,
                    chatHistory: chatHistory || [],
                };
            });
        });

        s.on("game:new_chat", (chatMsg: any) => {
            setGameState((prev) => {
                if (!prev) return prev;
                const history = prev.chatHistory || [];
                const isDuplicate = history.some(
                    (c) => c.timestamp === chatMsg.timestamp && c.message === chatMsg.message && c.userId === chatMsg.userId
                );
                if (isDuplicate) return prev;
                return {
                    ...prev,
                    chatHistory: [...history, chatMsg],
                };
            });
        });

        s.on("game:hint_result", ({ hint }: { hint: string }) => {
            toast.info(`💡 İPUCU: ${hint}`);
        });

        s.on("game:head_update", ({ userId, pitch, yaw }: any) => {
            setHeadRotations((prev) => ({ ...prev, [userId]: { pitch, yaw } }));
        });

        s.on("game:error", ({ message }: { message: string }) => {
            clearSubmitting();
            toast.error(message);
        });

        s.on("game:closed", () => {
            toast.error("Oda host tarafından kapatıldı!");
            s.disconnect();
            router.push("/");
        });

        s.on("game:clear_notepad", () => {
            if (optionsRef.current?.onClearNotepad) {
                optionsRef.current.onClearNotepad();
            }
            toast.error("🧠 AMNEZİ! Biri hafızanı sildi, tüm notların kayboldu!", { autoClose: 5000, theme: "colored" });
        });

        s.on("game:play_taunt", ({ userId, type }: any) => {
            const tauntId = Date.now();
            setTaunts((prev) => ({ ...prev, [userId]: { type, id: tauntId } }));
            setTimeout(() => {
                setTaunts((prev) => {
                    if (prev[userId]?.id === tauntId) {
                        const next = { ...prev };
                        delete next[userId];
                        return next;
                    }
                    return prev;
                });
            }, 2500);
        });

        s.on("game:visual_effect", async (payload: any) => {
            const audio = await import("@/utils/audio");
            if (payload.type === "bomb_explode") audio.playExplosionSound();
            else if (payload.type === "roulette_die") audio.playGunshotSound();
            else if (["mirror_cast", "shield_cast", "time_bend"].includes(payload.type)) audio.playMagicSound();
            else if (payload.type === "blood_tie") audio.playHeartbeatSound();
            else if (payload.type === "mind_wipe") audio.playGlitchSound();
            else if (payload.type === "silence_cast") audio.playSilenceSound();
            else if (payload.type === "extra_ammo") audio.playReloadSound();
            else if (payload.type === "letter_reveal") audio.playSparkleSound();

            const effId = Date.now() + Math.random();
            setVisualEffects((prev) => [...prev, { ...payload, id: effId }]);
            setTimeout(() => {
                setVisualEffects((prev) => prev.filter((e) => e.id !== effId));
            }, 2500);
        });
    }, [code, router]);

    useEffect(() => {
        if (!socket || !isConnected) return;
        socket.emit("game:ping", { clientTimestamp: Date.now() });

        const interval = setInterval(() => {
            socket.emit("game:ping", { clientTimestamp: Date.now() });
        }, 5000);

        return () => clearInterval(interval);
    }, [socket, isConnected]);


    useEffect(() => {
        const gameToken = localStorage.getItem("gameToken");
        if (!gameToken) {
            router.push(`/?invite=${code}`);
            return;
        }

        fetch(`${process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SERVER_URL || "http://localhost:4000"}/api/auth/me`, {
            headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${gameToken}` },
        })
            .then((res) => res.json())
            .then((data) => {
                if (data.username) {
                    connectToRoom(data);
                } else {
                    localStorage.removeItem("gameToken");
                    router.push(`/?invite=${code}`);
                }
            })
            .catch(() => {
                router.push(`/?invite=${code}`);
            });
    }, [code, router, connectToRoom]);

    // Socket temizleme
    useEffect(() => {
        return () => {
            if (socket) socket.disconnect();
        };
    }, [socket]);


    const handleStart = useCallback((settings: GameStartSettings) => {
        markSubmitting();
        socket?.emit("game:start", settings);
    }, [socket, markSubmitting]);

    const handleSetWord = useCallback((customWord?: string) => {
        const word = (typeof customWord === "string" ? customWord : wordInputRef.current).trim();
        if (!word) return;
        markSubmitting();
        socket?.emit("game:set_word", { word });
    }, [socket, markSubmitting]);

    const handleChat = useCallback((e?: React.FormEvent, customMsg?: string) => {
        e?.preventDefault();
        const msg = (typeof customMsg === "string" ? customMsg : chatInputRef.current).trim();
        if (!msg) return;
        socket?.emit("game:chat", { message: msg });
        setChatInput("");
    }, [socket]);

    const handleSkip = useCallback(() => {
        markSubmitting();
        socket?.emit("game:skip_turn");
    }, [socket, markSubmitting]);

    const handleAskQuestion = useCallback((question: string) => {
        markSubmitting();
        socket?.emit("game:ask_question", { question });
    }, [socket, markSubmitting]);

    const handleSubmitVote = useCallback((vote: string) => {
        markSubmitting();
        socket?.emit("game:submit_vote", { vote });
    }, [socket, markSubmitting]);

    const handleGuess = useCallback((customGuess?: string) => {
        const guess = (typeof customGuess === "string" ? customGuess : guessInputRef.current).trim();
        if (!guess) return;
        markSubmitting();
        socket?.emit("game:guess", { guess });
        setGuessInput("");
    }, [socket, markSubmitting]);

    const handleUseJoker = useCallback((payload: any) => {
        markSubmitting();
        socket?.emit("game:use_joker", payload);
    }, [socket, markSubmitting]);

    const toggleVoice = useCallback(async (enabled: boolean) => {
        socket?.emit("game:toggle_voice", { enabled });
    }, [socket]);

    const copyLink = useCallback(() => {
        navigator.clipboard.writeText(inviteUrl);
        toast.success("Davet linki kopyalandı!");
    }, [inviteUrl]);

    const handleLeaveRoom = useCallback(() => {
        socket?.emit("game:leave");
        if (socket) socket.disconnect();
        sessionStorage.removeItem("username");
        router.push("/");
    }, [socket, router]);

    const handleSelectAvatar = useCallback((id: number) => {
        socket?.emit("game:set_avatar", { avatarId: id });
    }, [socket]);

    const handlePlaceBet = useCallback((targetId: string) => {
        markSubmitting();
        socket?.emit("game:place_bet", { targetId });
    }, [socket, markSubmitting]);

    const handleEditWord = useCallback(() => {
        socket?.emit("game:edit_word");
    }, [socket]);

    const handleShuffleTargets = useCallback(() => {
        socket?.emit("game:shuffle_targets");
    }, [socket]);

    const handleSelectJoker = useCallback((jokerId: number) => {
        markSubmitting();
        socket?.emit("game:select_joker", { jokerId });
    }, [socket, markSubmitting]);

    const handleStartObjection = useCallback(() => {
        markSubmitting();
        socket?.emit("game:start_objection");
    }, [socket, markSubmitting]);

    const handleVoteObjection = useCallback((vote: boolean) => {
        markSubmitting();
        socket?.emit("game:vote_objection", { vote });
    }, [socket, markSubmitting]);

    const handleHeadRotation = useCallback((pitch: number, yaw: number) => {
        socket?.emit("game:head_rotation", { pitch, yaw });
    }, [socket]);

    const handleTaunt = useCallback((type: string) => {
        socket?.emit("game:taunt", { type });
    }, [socket]);

    const handleCloseRoom = useCallback(() => {
        toast(
            ({ closeToast }) => (
                <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
                    <p className="font-bold">Odayı kapatmak istediğine emin misin?</p>
                    <p className="text-sm">Odada bulunan herkes atılacak.</p>
                    <div className="flex gap-2 justify-end mt-2">
                        <Button size="small" variant="outlined" color="inherit" onClick={closeToast}>
                            İptal
                        </Button>
                        <Button
                            size="small"
                            variant="contained"
                            color="error"
                            onClick={() => {
                                socket?.emit("room:close");
                                if (socket) socket.disconnect();
                                sessionStorage.removeItem("username");
                                router.push("/");
                                if (closeToast) closeToast();
                            }}
                        >
                            Kapat
                        </Button>
                    </div>
                </div>
            ),
            { autoClose: false, closeOnClick: false, draggable: false, position: "top-center" }
        );
    }, [socket, router]);

    const actions: GameActions = useMemo(() => ({
        start: handleStart,
        setWord: handleSetWord,
        chat: handleChat,
        skip: handleSkip,
        askQuestion: handleAskQuestion,
        submitVote: handleSubmitVote,
        guess: handleGuess,
        useJoker: handleUseJoker,
        toggleVoice,
        copyLink,
        leaveRoom: handleLeaveRoom,
        selectAvatar: handleSelectAvatar,
        placeBet: handlePlaceBet,
        editWord: handleEditWord,
        shuffleTargets: handleShuffleTargets,
        selectJoker: handleSelectJoker,
        startObjection: handleStartObjection,
        voteObjection: handleVoteObjection,
        headRotation: handleHeadRotation,
        taunt: handleTaunt,
        closeRoom: handleCloseRoom,
    }), [
        handleStart,
        handleSetWord,
        handleChat,
        handleSkip,
        handleAskQuestion,
        handleSubmitVote,
        handleGuess,
        handleUseJoker,
        toggleVoice,
        copyLink,
        handleLeaveRoom,
        handleSelectAvatar,
        handlePlaceBet,
        handleEditWord,
        handleShuffleTargets,
        handleSelectJoker,
        handleStartObjection,
        handleVoteObjection,
        handleHeadRotation,
        handleTaunt,
        handleCloseRoom,
    ]);

    return {
       
        socket,
        gameState,
        inviteUrl,
        isJoined,
        checkingAuth,
        myId,
        me,
        headRotations,
        taunts,
        visualEffects,

        isConnected,
        isReconnecting,
        pingMs,
        connectionError,
        isSubmitting,

        // Inputs
        wordInput,
        setWordInput,
        chatInput,
        setChatInput,
        guessDialogOpen,
        setGuessDialogOpen,
        guessInput,
        setGuessInput,

        actions,

        handleStart,
        handleSetWord,
        handleChat,
        handleSkip,
        handleAskQuestion,
        handleSubmitVote,
        handleGuess,
        handleUseJoker,
        toggleVoice,
        copyLink,
        handleLeaveRoom,
        handleSelectAvatar,
        handleCloseRoom,
        handlePlaceBet,
        handleEditWord,
        handleShuffleTargets,
        handleSelectJoker,
        handleStartObjection,
        handleVoteObjection,
        handleHeadRotation,
        handleTaunt,
    };
}


=========================
DOSYA: client\hooks\useLiveKit.ts
=========================

"use client";

import { useState, useEffect } from "react";

export interface UseLiveKitResult {
    token: string;
    isLoading: boolean;
    error: string | null;
}

export function useLiveKit(
    room?: string | string[],
    identity?: string
): UseLiveKitResult {
    const [token, setToken] = useState<string>("");
    const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState<boolean>(false);
    const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

    useEffect(() => {
        if (!room || !identity) {
            setToken("");
            return;
        }

        let isCancelled = false;

        const fetchToken = async () => {
            setIsLoading(true);
            setError(null);
            try {
                const gameToken = typeof window !== "undefined" ? localStorage.getItem("gameToken") : null;
                const roomStr = Array.isArray(room) ? room[0] : room;
                const resp = await fetch(
                    `/api/livekit?room=${encodeURIComponent(roomStr)}&username=${encodeURIComponent(identity)}`,
                    {
                        headers: gameToken ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${gameToken}` } : {},
                    }
                );

                if (!resp.ok) {
                    throw new Error(`LiveKit token alma hatası: ${resp.statusText}`);
                }

                const data = await resp.json();
                if (!isCancelled && data.token) {
                    setToken(data.token);
                }
            } catch (err: any) {
                if (!isCancelled) {
                    console.error("[useLiveKit] Token fetch error:", err);
                    setError(err?.message || "LiveKit token alınamadı");
                }
            } finally {
                if (!isCancelled) {
                    setIsLoading(false);
                }
            }
        };

        fetchToken();

        return () => {
            isCancelled = true;
        };
    }, [room, identity]);

    return { token, isLoading, error };
}


=========================
DOSYA: client\hooks\useNotepad.ts
=========================

"use client";

import { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from "react";

export interface UseNotepadResult {
    notepad: string;
    setNotepad: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<string>>;
    clearNotepad: () => void;
    isLoaded: boolean;
}

export function useNotepad(code?: string | string[]): UseNotepadResult {
    const [notepad, setNotepad] = useState<string>("");
    const [isLoaded, setIsLoaded] = useState<boolean>(false);

    const roomCode = Array.isArray(code) ? code[0] : code;
    const storageKey = roomCode ? `kimbuki_notepad_${roomCode}` : null;

    useEffect(() => {
        if (!storageKey || typeof window === "undefined") return;
        try {
            const saved = localStorage.getItem(storageKey);
            if (saved !== null) {
                setNotepad(saved);
            }
        } catch (e) {
            console.error("[useNotepad] LocalStorage okuma hatası:", e);
        } finally {
            setIsLoaded(true);
        }
    }, [storageKey]);

    useEffect(() => {
        if (!isLoaded || !storageKey || typeof window === "undefined") return;
        try {
            localStorage.setItem(storageKey, notepad);
        } catch (e) {
            console.error("[useNotepad] LocalStorage yazma hatası:", e);
        }
    }, [notepad, storageKey, isLoaded]);

    // Hafıza silindiğinde (Amnezi jokeri vb.) notları temizleme
    const clearNotepad = useCallback(() => {
        setNotepad("");
        if (storageKey && typeof window !== "undefined") {
            try {
                localStorage.removeItem(storageKey);
            } catch (e) {
                console.error("[useNotepad] LocalStorage temizleme hatası:", e);
            }
        }
    }, [storageKey]);

    return {
        notepad,
        setNotepad,
        clearNotepad,
        isLoaded,
    };
}

DOSYA: client\utils\audio.ts
