const BaseJoker = require('./BaseJoker');

class LifeSwap extends BaseJoker {
    constructor() {
        super(13, "Can Takası", "prismatic");
    }

    execute(room, user, payload, context) {
        const targetId = context.targetId;
        const initialTargetId = context.initialTargetId;

        if (!targetId || typeof targetId !== 'string') return false;

        const target = room.users.find(u => u.id === targetId);
        if (!target || target.status !== 'playing') return false;

        if (target.id === user.id && initialTargetId !== user.id) {
            room.io.to(room.code).emit("game:visual_effect", { type: 'mind_wipe', targetId: user.id }); 
            room.addChatLog({ system: true, message: `🤡 BÜYÜ GERİ TEPTİ! Ayna yüzünden ${user.name} kendi canını kendisiyle takas etti. Joker tamamen boşa gitti!`, timestamp: Date.now() });
        } else if (target.shieldActive) {
            target.shieldActive = false;
            room.io.to(room.code).emit("game:visual_effect", { type: 'shield_cast', targetId: target.id });
            room.addChatLog({ system: true, message: `🧛‍♂️ ${user.name} Can Takası yapmak istedi ama ${target.name}'in Gizli Kalkanı buna engel oldu!`, timestamp: Date.now() });
        } else {
            const tempLives = user.lives;
            user.lives = target.lives;
            target.lives = tempLives;

            room.io.to(room.code).emit("game:visual_effect", { type: 'blood_tie', targetId: target.id });
            room.io.to(room.code).emit("game:visual_effect", { type: 'blood_tie', targetId: user.id });

            room.addChatLog({ system: true, message: `🧛‍♂️ İHANET! ${user.name}, ${target.name} ile canlarını takas etti!`, timestamp: Date.now() });

            if (user.lives <= 0) {
                user.status = 'spectator';
                room.addChatLog({ system: true, message: `💀 ${user.name}, canı olmayan biriyle takas yapıp kendi kendini eledi!`, timestamp: Date.now() });
            }
            if (target.lives <= 0) {
                target.status = 'spectator';
                room.addChatLog({ system: true, message: `💀 ${target.name} canı kalmadığı için elendi! Kelimesi: ${target.assignedWord}`, timestamp: Date.now() });
            }
        }
        return true;
    }
}

module.exports = LifeSwap;


=========================
DOSYA: server\game\jokers\MindWiper.js
=========================

const BaseJoker = require('./BaseJoker');

class MindWiper extends BaseJoker {
    constructor() {
        super(4, "Hafıza Silici", "prismatic");
    }

    execute(room, user, payload, context) {
        const targetId = context.targetId;
        const initialTargetId = context.initialTargetId;

        if (!targetId || !payload.newWord || typeof payload.newWord !== 'string') {
            return false;
        }

        const cleanWord = payload.newWord.trim().substring(0, 30);
        if (!cleanWord) {
            room.io.to(user.id).emit("game:error", { message: "Geçerli bir kelime girmelisiniz!" });
            return false;
        }

        const target = room.users.find(u => u.id === targetId);
        if (!target) return false;

        // Ayna geri sekmesi kontrolü
        if (target.id === user.id && initialTargetId !== user.id) {
            room.pendingMirrorWordOwner = initialTargetId;
            room.pendingMirrorWordTarget = user.id;
            target.assignedWord = "AYNA SAHİBİ KELİME BEKLENİYOR"; 
            target.revealedLetters = [];
            
            const mirrorOwner = room.users.find(u => u.id === initialTargetId);
            room.addChatLog({ system: true, message: `🧠 Hafıza Silici geri tepti! ${target.name}'in kelimesi silindi! Ayna sahibi ${mirrorOwner ? mirrorOwner.name : ''} yeni kelimeyi belirliyor...`, timestamp: Date.now() });
            room.addChatLog({ system: true, privateTo: initialTargetId, message: `Sistem (Özel): Ayna ile Hafıza Siliciyi yansıttın! Lütfen 30 saniye içinde chat alanına "/kelime YENIKELIME" yazarak rakibinin yeni kelimesini belirle (Örn: /kelime Karpuz).`, timestamp: Date.now() });

            if (room.mirrorWordTimer) clearTimeout(room.mirrorWordTimer);
            room.mirrorWordTimer = setTimeout(() => {
                if (room.pendingMirrorWordOwner) {
                    const mirrorOwnerStillHere = room.users.find(u => u.id === room.pendingMirrorWordOwner && !u.disconnected);
                    const t = room.users.find(u => u.id === room.pendingMirrorWordTarget);
                    if (t) {
                        if (!mirrorOwnerStillHere) {
                            t.assignedWord = "BİLİNMEYEN";
                            room.addChatLog({ system: true, message: `⏳ Ayna sahibi oyundan koptuğu için ${t.name}'in kelimesi sıfırlandı!`, timestamp: Date.now() });
                        } else {
                            t.assignedWord = Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 8).toUpperCase();
                            room.addChatLog({ system: true, message: `⏳ Ayna sahibi süresinde kelime belirlemedi! ${t.name}'in kelimesi rastgele harflere dönüştü!`, timestamp: Date.now() });
                        }
                        room.broadcastState();
                    }
                    room.pendingMirrorWordOwner = null;
                    room.pendingMirrorWordTarget = null;
                }
            }, 30000);
        } else {
            target.assignedWord = cleanWord; 
            room.addChatLog({ system: true, message: `🧠 Hafıza Silici kullanıldı! ${target.name}'in kelimesi acımasızca değiştirildi!`, timestamp: Date.now() });
            target.revealedLetters = [];
            room.io.to(room.code).emit("game:visual_effect", { type: 'mind_wipe', targetId: target.id });
        }

        return true;
    }
}

module.exports = MindWiper;


=========================
DOSYA: server\game\jokers\MirrorShield.js
=========================

const BaseJoker = require('./BaseJoker');

class MirrorShield extends BaseJoker {
    constructor() {
        super(8, "Ayna Kalkanı", "gold");
    }

    execute(room, user, payload, context) {
        user.mirrorRoundsLeft = 3;
        room.addChatLog({ system: true, privateTo: user.id, message: "Sistem (Özel): Ayna (Kalkan) aktif! 3 tur boyunca sana atılan kötü niyetli jokerler atan kişiye geri sekecek.", timestamp: Date.now() });
        room.io.to(user.id).emit("game:visual_effect", { type: 'mirror_cast', targetId: user.id });
        return true;
    }
}

module.exports = MirrorShield;

const BaseJoker = require('./BaseJoker');

class MysteryBox extends BaseJoker {
    constructor(id, name, rarity, targetPool) {
        super(id, name, rarity);
        this.targetPool = targetPool;
    }

    execute(room, user, payload, context) {
        const chosenJoker = this.targetPool[Math.floor(Math.random() * this.targetPool.length)];
        user.joker = chosenJoker;
        const rarityText = this.id === 11 ? "ALTIN" : "PRİZMATİK";
        room.addChatLog({
            system: true,
            privateTo: user.id,
            message: `Sistem (Özel): ${this.name}'yu açtın ve içinden ${rarityText} joker çıktı!`,
            timestamp: Date.now()
        });
        user.hasUsedJoker = false;
        return true;
    }
}

module.exports = MysteryBox;

const BaseJoker = require('./BaseJoker');

class Telepathy extends BaseJoker {
    constructor() {
        super(16, "Telepati", "common");
    }

    execute(room, user, payload, context) {
        const targetId = context.targetId;
        if (targetId && typeof targetId === 'string') {
            const target = room.users.find(u => u.id === targetId);
            if (target && target.status === 'playing' && user.assignedWord && target.assignedWord) {
                const myLetters = [...new Set(user.assignedWord.toLocaleUpperCase("tr-TR").replace(/[^A-ZÇĞİÖŞÜ0-9]/g, "").split(''))];
                const targetLetters = [...new Set(target.assignedWord.toLocaleUpperCase("tr-TR").replace(/[^A-ZÇĞİÖŞÜ0-9]/g, "").split(''))];
                
                const commonLetters = myLetters.filter(l => targetLetters.includes(l));

                room.io.to(room.code).emit("game:visual_effect", { type: 'letter_reveal', targetId: user.id });
                room.io.to(room.code).emit("game:visual_effect", { type: 'letter_reveal', targetId: target.id });
                room.addChatLog({ system: true, message: `🤝 TELEPATİ! ${user.name} ve ${target.name} zihinlerini birleştirdi!`, timestamp: Date.now() });

                if (commonLetters.length > 0) {
                    const commonStr = commonLetters.join(", ");
                    room.addChatLog({ system: true, privateTo: user.id, message: `Sistem (Özel): ${target.name} ile kelimelerinizdeki ORTAK HARFLER şunlar: ${commonStr}`, timestamp: Date.now() });
                    room.addChatLog({ system: true, privateTo: target.id, message: `Sistem (Özel): ${user.name} ile kelimelerinizdeki ORTAK HARFLER şunlar: ${commonStr}`, timestamp: Date.now() });
                    
                    commonLetters.forEach(char => {
                        if (!user.revealedLetters) user.revealedLetters = [];
                        if (!target.revealedLetters) target.revealedLetters = [];
                        if (!user.revealedLetters.includes(char)) user.revealedLetters.push(char);
                        if (!target.revealedLetters.includes(char)) target.revealedLetters.push(char);
                    });
                } else {
                    room.addChatLog({ system: true, privateTo: user.id, message: `Sistem (Özel): ${target.name} ile hiçbir ortak harfiniz YOK!`, timestamp: Date.now() });
                    room.addChatLog({ system: true, privateTo: target.id, message: `Sistem (Özel): ${user.name} ile hiçbir ortak harfiniz YOK!`, timestamp: Date.now() });
                }

                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }
}

module.exports = Telepathy;

const BaseJoker = require('./BaseJoker');

class TimeBender extends BaseJoker {
    constructor() {
        super(0, "Zaman Bükücü", "common");
    }

    execute(room, user, payload, context) {
        if (room.currentTurnIndex !== -1 && room.users[room.currentTurnIndex].id === user.id) {
            if (room.timer) clearTimeout(room.timer);
            room.turnTimeLeft = (room.turnStartTime + (room.turnTime * 1000) - Date.now()) / 1000;
            const newTime = room.turnTimeLeft + 60;
            room.turnStartTime = Date.now() - (room.turnTime * 1000 - newTime * 1000); 
            room.timer = setTimeout(() => { if (room.gameState === "PLAYING") room.nextTurn(); }, newTime * 1000);
            room.addChatLog({ system: true, message: `⏳ ${user.name} Zaman Bükücü kullanarak süresine 1 dakika ekledi!`, timestamp: Date.now() });
            room.io.to(room.code).emit("game:visual_effect", { type: 'time_bend', targetId: user.id });
            return true;
        }
        return false;
    }
}

module.exports = TimeBender;

            if (target) {
                const actualCaster = (targetId === user.id) ? initialTargetId : user.id;
                target.timeBomb = { casterId: actualCaster, roundsLeft: 3 };
                room.addChatLog({ system: true, message: `💣 SAATLİ BOMBA! ${target.name}'in üzerine bomba yerleştirildi. 3 tur içinde bilemezse patlayacak!`, timestamp: Date.now() });

const BaseJoker = require('./BaseJoker');

class WordComparator extends BaseJoker {
    constructor() {
        super(10, "Boyut Ölçer", "common");
    }

    execute(room, user, payload, context) {
        const targetId = context.targetId;
        if (targetId && typeof targetId === 'string') {
            const target = room.users.find(u => u.id === targetId);
            if (target && user.assignedWord && target.assignedWord) {
                const mLen = user.assignedWord.replace(/\s/g, '').length;
                const tLen = target.assignedWord.replace(/\s/g, '').length;
                const cmp = mLen > tLen ? "DAHA UZUN" : mLen < tLen ? "DAHA KISA" : "AYNI UZUNLUKTA";
                room.addChatLog({ system: true, privateTo: user.id, message: `Sistem (Özel): Senin kelimen ${target.name}'in kelimesinden ${cmp}.`, timestamp: Date.now() });
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }
}

=========================
DOSYA: server\game\jokers\index.js
=========================

const RewardService = require('../services/RewardService');

const TimeBender = require('./TimeBender');
const LetterOpener = require('./LetterOpener');
const DoubleMagazine = require('./DoubleMagazine');
const Silencer = require('./Silencer');
const MindWiper = require('./MindWiper');
const BloodTie = require('./BloodTie');
const RussianRoulette = require('./RussianRoulette');
const TimeBomb = require('./TimeBomb');
const MirrorShield = require('./MirrorShield');
const SecretShield = require('./SecretShield');
const WordComparator = require('./WordComparator');
const MysteryBox = require('./MysteryBox');
const LifeSwap = require('./LifeSwap');
const LetterDetective = require('./LetterDetective');
const Amnesia = require('./Amnesia');
const Telepathy = require('./Telepathy');

class JokerManager {
    constructor() {
        this.strategies = new Map();
        this.registerStrategies();
    }

    registerStrategies() {
        this.strategies.set(0, new TimeBender());
        this.strategies.set(1, new LetterOpener());
        this.strategies.set(2, new DoubleMagazine());
        this.strategies.set(3, new Silencer());
        this.strategies.set(4, new MindWiper());
        this.strategies.set(5, new BloodTie());
        this.strategies.set(6, new RussianRoulette());
        this.strategies.set(7, new TimeBomb());
        this.strategies.set(8, new MirrorShield());
        this.strategies.set(9, new SecretShield());
        this.strategies.set(10, new WordComparator());
        this.strategies.set(11, new MysteryBox(11, "Gümüş Sürpriz Kutu", "silver", [2, 3, 8, 12, 15]));
        this.strategies.set(12, new MysteryBox(12, "Altın Sürpriz Kutu", "gold", [4, 5, 6, 7, 13]));
        this.strategies.set(13, new LifeSwap());
        this.strategies.set(14, new LetterDetective());
        this.strategies.set(15, new Amnesia());
        this.strategies.set(16, new Telepathy());
    }

    /**
     * Executes a joker action for a user in a room.
     * @param {object} room - Room instance
     * @param {string} userId - User socket ID
     * @param {object} payload - Action parameters (targetId, newWord, etc.)
     */
    executeJoker(room, userId, payload) {
        if (room.gameState !== "PLAYING" || !payload || typeof payload !== 'object') return false;

        if (room.roundCount < 3) {
            room.io.to(userId).emit("game:error", { message: "Jokerler 3. turdan itibaren kullanılabilir!" });
            return false;
        }

        const user = room.users.find(u => u.id === userId);
        if (!user || user.status !== 'playing' || user.hasUsedJoker || user.joker === null) return false;

        if (room.users[room.currentTurnIndex]?.id !== userId) {
            room.io.to(userId).emit("game:error", { message: "Jokerini sadece kendi sıranda kullanabilirsin!" });
            return false;
        }

        const jokerType = user.joker;
        const strategy = this.strategies.get(jokerType);
        if (!strategy) {
            room.io.to(userId).emit("game:error", { message: "Bilinmeyen joker türü!" });
            return false;
        }

        let targetId = payload.targetId;
        const initialTargetId = targetId;

        const forbiddenSelfTargets = [3, 4, 5, 7, 10, 13, 15, 16];
        if (targetId === user.id && forbiddenSelfTargets.includes(jokerType)) {
            room.io.to(userId).emit("game:error", { message: "Bu jokeri kendi üzerinde kullanamazsın!" });
            return false;
        }

        if (jokerType === 7 && targetId && typeof targetId === 'string') {
            const targetForBomb = room.users.find(u => u.id === targetId);
            if (targetForBomb && targetForBomb.timeBomb) {
                room.io.to(userId).emit("game:error", { message: "Bu oyuncunun üzerinde zaten aktif bir bomba var!" });
                return false;
            }
        }

        const reflectableJokers = [3, 4, 5, 7, 13, 15];
        if (targetId && reflectableJokers.includes(jokerType)) {
            const targetUser = room.users.find(u => u.id === targetId);
            if (targetUser && targetUser.mirrorRoundsLeft > 0 && targetUser.id !== user.id) {
                room.addChatLog({
                    system: true,
                    message: `🛡️ YANSIMA! Kötü niyetli joker, gizli Ayna Kalkanı'na çarpıp ${user.name}'e geri sekti!`,
                    timestamp: Date.now()
                });
                targetId = user.id;
            }
        }

        const context = { initialTargetId, targetId };
        const jokerConsumed = strategy.execute(room, user, payload, context);

        if (jokerConsumed) {
            if (jokerType !== 11 && jokerType !== 12 && jokerType !== 14) {
                user.hasUsedJoker = true;
            } else if (jokerType === 14 && user.detectiveUses >= 3) {
                user.hasUsedJoker = true;
            }

            if (user.dbId) {
                RewardService.progressQuest(user.dbId, 'use_joker');
            }
        } else if (jokerType !== 14 || (user.detectiveUses === undefined || user.detectiveUses === 0)) {
            room.io.to(userId).emit("game:error", { message: "Joker geçersiz bir hedefe uygulanamadı. Jokerin iade edildi!" });
        }

        room.broadcastState();
        return jokerConsumed;
    }
}

module.exports = new JokerManager();


=========================
DOSYA: server\game\services\RewardService.js
=========================

const User = require('../../models/User');

class RewardService {
    /**
     * Atomically updates user gold and reputation, and progresses active quests.
     * @param {string} dbId MongoDB user ID
     * @param {Object} rewards { gold, rep, winCount, playCount, betWinCount }
     */
    static async grantUserRewards(dbId, { gold = 0, rep = 0, winCount = 0, playCount = 0, betWinCount = 0 } = {}) {
        if (!dbId) return null;

        try {
            const incPayload = {};
            if (gold !== 0) incPayload.gold = gold;
            if (rep !== 0) incPayload.reputation = rep;

            const updateQuery = Object.keys(incPayload).length > 0 ? { $inc: incPayload } : {};
            const userDb = await User.findOneAndUpdate(
                { _id: dbId },
                updateQuery,
                { new: true }
            );

            if (userDb && userDb.quests && userDb.quests.active) {
                let modified = false;
                userDb.quests.active.forEach(q => {
                    if (q.type === 'win' && !q.isClaimed && q.progress < q.target && winCount > 0) {
                        q.progress += winCount;
                        modified = true;
                    }
                    if (q.type === 'play' && !q.isClaimed && q.progress < q.target && playCount > 0) {
                        q.progress += playCount;
                        modified = true;
                    }
                    if (q.type === 'bet_win' && !q.isClaimed && q.progress < q.target && betWinCount > 0) {
                        q.progress += betWinCount;
                        modified = true;
                    }
                });

                if (modified) {
                    userDb.markModified('quests');
                    await userDb.save();
                }
            }

            return userDb;
        } catch (error) {
            console.error(`[RewardService] Error granting rewards to user ${dbId}:`, error);
            return null;
        }
    }

    /**
     * Grants rewards in batch to multiple users concurrently.
     * @param {Object} playerRewards Map of dbId -> { gold, rep, winCount, playCount, betWinCount }
     */
    static async grantBatchRewards(playerRewards) {
        if (!playerRewards || typeof playerRewards !== 'object') return [];
        const entries = Object.entries(playerRewards);
        const promises = entries.map(([dbId, rewards]) => this.grantUserRewards(dbId, rewards));
        return Promise.all(promises);
    }

    /**
     * Grants participation rewards to non-winning players at the end of a round.
     * @param {Array} users List of room users
     * @param {Array} winnerIds List of winning socket IDs
     * @param {number} participationRep Default: 5
     */
    static async grantParticipationRewards(users, winnerIds = [], participationRep = 5) {
        if (!Array.isArray(users)) return [];

        const promises = users
            .filter(u => u.dbId && !winnerIds.includes(u.id))
            .map(u => {
                u.reputation = (u.reputation || 0) + participationRep;
                return this.grantUserRewards(u.dbId, { rep: participationRep, playCount: 1 });
            });

        return Promise.all(promises);
    }

    /**
     * Atomically deducts entry bet amount from all active players.
     * If any player has insufficient gold or fails, rolls back all deducted players.
     * @param {Array} activeUsers List of active user objects
     * @param {number} betAmount Entry bet amount
     * @returns {Object} { ok: boolean, failedUser: Object | null }
     */
    static async deductBetFees(activeUsers, betAmount) {
        if (!betAmount || betAmount <= 0) return { ok: true, failedUser: null };
        const deductedUserDbIds = [];

        for (let u of activeUsers) {
            if (u.dbId) {
                try {
                    const updated = await User.findOneAndUpdate(
                        { _id: u.dbId, gold: { $gte: betAmount } },
                        { $inc: { gold: -betAmount } },
                        { new: true }
                    );
                    if (!updated) {
                        await this.refundBetFees(deductedUserDbIds, betAmount);
                        return { ok: false, failedUser: u };
                    }
                    deductedUserDbIds.push(u.dbId);
                } catch (err) {
                    console.error(`[RewardService] Error deducting bet fee for ${u.name}:`, err);
                    await this.refundBetFees(deductedUserDbIds, betAmount);
                    return { ok: false, failedUser: u };
                }
            } else {
                await this.refundBetFees(deductedUserDbIds, betAmount);
                return { ok: false, failedUser: u };
            }
        }
        return { ok: true, failedUser: null };
    }

    /**
     * Refunds deducted bet fees in case of rollback or cancelled game.
     * @param {Array} dbIds List of user dbIds
     * @param {number} betAmount
     */
    static async refundBetFees(dbIds, betAmount) {
        if (!Array.isArray(dbIds) || dbIds.length === 0 || !betAmount) return;
        const promises = dbIds.map(id =>
            User.findByIdAndUpdate(id, { $inc: { gold: betAmount } }).catch(e => console.error(e))
        );
        await Promise.all(promises);
    }
    /**
     * Atomically progresses a specific quest type for a user.
     * @param {string} dbId
     * @param {string} questType
     * @param {number} amount
     */
    static async progressQuest(dbId, questType, amount = 1) {
        if (!dbId || !questType) return null;
        try {
            const userDb = await User.findById(dbId);
            if (userDb && userDb.quests && userDb.quests.active) {
                let modified = false;
                userDb.quests.active.forEach(q => {
                    if (q.type === questType && !q.isClaimed && q.progress < q.target) {
                        q.progress += amount;
                        modified = true;
                    }
                });
                if (modified) {
                    userDb.markModified('quests');
                    await userDb.save();
                }
            }
            return userDb;
        } catch (err) {
            console.error(`[RewardService] Error progressing quest ${questType} for user ${dbId}:`, err);
            return null;
        }
    }

    /**
     * Fetches user reputation with lean/select query.
     * @param {string} userId
     * @returns {number}
     */
    static async getUserReputation(userId) {
        if (!userId) return 0;
        try {
            const user = await User.findById(userId).select('reputation').lean();
            return user?.reputation || 0;
        } catch {
            return 0;
        }
    }

    /**
     * Calculates winner gold, bettor gold, and reputation rewards based on player count, rank, and game mode.
     * @param {Object} params
     * @param {boolean} params.isBettingEnabled
     * @param {number} params.totalPot
     * @param {number} params.playersCount
     * @param {number} params.rank
     * @param {boolean} params.isRussianRoulette
     * @returns {Object} { winnerReward, bettorReward, repReward }
     */
    static calculatePotDistribution({ isBettingEnabled = false, totalPot = 0, playersCount = 2, rank = 1, isRussianRoulette = false }) {
        let winnerReward = 0;
        let bettorReward = 0;

        if (isBettingEnabled) {
            if (isRussianRoulette) {
                winnerReward = totalPot;
                bettorReward = 0;
            } else {
                let pct = 0;
                if (playersCount <= 2) {
                    pct = rank === 1 ? 1.0 : 0;
                } else if (playersCount === 3) {
                    pct = rank === 1 ? 0.60 : (rank === 2 ? 0.40 : 0);
                } else if (playersCount === 4) {
                    pct = rank === 1 ? 0.50 : (rank === 2 ? 0.30 : (rank === 3 ? 0.20 : 0));
                } else {
                    if (rank === 1) pct = 0.40;
                    else if (rank === 2) pct = 0.30;
                    else if (rank === 3) pct = 0.20;
                    else if (rank === 4) pct = 0.10;
                    else pct = 0;
                }
                winnerReward = Math.floor(totalPot * pct);
                bettorReward = Math.max(1, Math.floor(winnerReward / 2));
            }
        }

        let repReward = 5;
        if (isRussianRoulette) repReward = 200;
        else if (rank === 1) repReward = 50;
        else if (rank === 2) repReward = 20;
        else if (rank === 3) repReward = 10;

        return {
            winnerReward,
            bettorReward,
            repReward
        };
    }
}

module.exports = RewardService;


=========================
DOSYA: server\game\validators\payloadValidator.js
=========================


const chatRateLimits = new Map();       
const headRotRateLimits = new Map();    
const tauntRateLimits = new Map();      
const actionRateLimits = new Map();     


setInterval(() => {
    const now = Date.now();
    for (const [id, timestamps] of chatRateLimits.entries()) {
        const recent = timestamps.filter(t => now - t < 5000);
        if (recent.length === 0) chatRateLimits.delete(id);
        else chatRateLimits.set(id, recent);
    }
    for (const [id, data] of headRotRateLimits.entries()) {
        if (now - data.windowStart > 5000) headRotRateLimits.delete(id);
    }
    for (const [id, lastTime] of tauntRateLimits.entries()) {
        if (now - lastTime > 10000) tauntRateLimits.delete(id);
    }
    for (const [key, lastTime] of actionRateLimits.entries()) {
        if (now - lastTime > 10000) actionRateLimits.delete(key);
    }
}, 30000);

const payloadValidator = {
   
    checkActionRateLimit(userId, action, cooldownMs = 500) {
        if (!userId) return { ok: true };
        const key = `${userId}:${action}`;
        const now = Date.now();
        const lastTime = actionRateLimits.get(key) || 0;
        if (now - lastTime < cooldownMs) {
            return { ok: false, error: "Çok hızlı işlem yapıyorsunuz! Lütfen biraz bekleyin." };
        }
        actionRateLimits.set(key, now);
        return { ok: true };
    },

    
    validateActivePlayer(user, room) {
        if (!room) {
            return { ok: false, error: "Oda bulunamadı." };
        }
        if (!user) {
            return { ok: false, error: "Oyuncu profili bulunamadı." };
        }
        if (user.disconnected) {
            return { ok: false, error: "Bağlantınız kopmuş durumda. Lütfen odaya tekrar bağlanın." };
        }
        if (user.status !== "playing") {
            return { ok: false, error: "Yalnızca hayatta olan aktif oyuncular bu aksiyonu gerçekleştirebilir. İzleyiciler (spectator) oyun akışını değiştiremez!" };
        }
        return { ok: true };
    },

   
    validateCurrentTurn(user, room, actionName = "aksiyon almak") {
        const activeCheck = this.validateActivePlayer(user, room);
        if (!activeCheck.ok) return activeCheck;

        if (room.gameState !== "PLAYING") {
            return { ok: false, error: `Şu anda oyun aşamasında değilsiniz. (Mevcut aşama: ${room.gameState})` };
        }
        const currentUser = room.users[room.currentTurnIndex];
        if (!currentUser || currentUser.id !== user.id) {
            return { ok: false, error: `Sıra sizde değil! ${actionName} için sıranın size gelmesini bekleyin.` };
        }
        return { ok: true };
    },

  
    validateRoomCreate(payload) {
        if (!payload || typeof payload !== "object") {
            return { ok: false, error: "Geçersiz istek gövdesi." };
        }
        const name = typeof payload.name === "string" ? payload.name.trim() : "";
        if (name.length < 2 || name.length > 20) {
            return { ok: false, error: "Oyuncu adı 2 ile 20 karakter arasında olmalıdır." };
        }

        return {
            ok: true,
            data: {
                name,
                avatar: typeof payload.avatar === "string" || typeof payload.avatar === "number" ? payload.avatar : "Warrior",
                pedestal: typeof payload.pedestal === "string" ? payload.pedestal.substring(0, 30) : "default_stone",
                nameColor: typeof payload.nameColor === "string" ? payload.nameColor.substring(0, 30) : "text-white",
                title: typeof payload.title === "string" ? payload.title.substring(0, 40) : "",
            }
        };
    },

    
    validateRoomJoin(payload) {
        if (!payload || typeof payload !== "object") {
            return { ok: false, error: "Geçersiz istek gövdesi." };
        }
        const rawCode = typeof payload.roomCode === "string" ? payload.roomCode.trim().toUpperCase() : "";
        if (!/^[A-Z0-9]{4,10}$/.test(rawCode)) {
            return { ok: false, error: "Geçersiz oda kodu formatı." };
        }

        const name = typeof payload.name === "string" ? payload.name.trim() : "";
        if (name.length < 2 || name.length > 20) {
            return { ok: false, error: "Oyuncu adı 2 ile 20 karakter arasında olmalıdır." };
        }

        return {
            ok: true,
            data: {
                roomCode: rawCode,
                name,
                avatar: typeof payload.avatar === "string" || typeof payload.avatar === "number" ? payload.avatar : "Warrior",
                pedestal: typeof payload.pedestal === "string" ? payload.pedestal.substring(0, 30) : "default_stone",
                nameColor: typeof payload.nameColor === "string" ? payload.nameColor.substring(0, 30) : "text-white",
                title: typeof payload.title === "string" ? payload.title.substring(0, 40) : "",
            }
        };
    },

    
    validateGameStart(settings, user, room) {
        if (!user || !user.isHost) {
            return { ok: false, error: "Yalnızca oda kurucusu (Host) oyunu başlatabilir!" };
        }
        if (!room) {
            return { ok: false, error: "Oda bulunamadı." };
        }
        if (room.gameState !== "LOBBY") {
            return { ok: false, error: "Oyun zaten başlamış durumda!" };
        }
        const activeUsers = room.users.filter(u => !u.disconnected);
        if (activeUsers.length < 2) {
            return { ok: false, error: "Oyunu başlatmak için en az 2 aktif oyuncu gereklidir." };
        }

        const rawSettings = settings && typeof settings === "object" ? settings : {};
        const betAmount = Number(rawSettings.betAmount);
        const validBet = Number.isInteger(betAmount) && betAmount >= 10 && betAmount <= 100000 ? betAmount : 50;

        return {
            ok: true,
            data: {
                category: typeof rawSettings.category === "string" ? rawSettings.category.substring(0, 30) : "Karışık",
                theme: rawSettings.theme === "night" ? "night" : "day",
                map: typeof rawSettings.map === "string" ? rawSettings.map.substring(0, 60) : "floating_island__low_poly_vr.glb",
                bettingEnabled: Boolean(rawSettings.bettingEnabled),
                jokersEnabled: rawSettings.jokersEnabled !== undefined ? Boolean(rawSettings.jokersEnabled) : true,
                betAmount: validBet,
            }
        };
    },

   
    validateSetWord(word, user, room) {
        const activeCheck = this.validateActivePlayer(user, room);
        if (!activeCheck.ok) return activeCheck;

        if (room.gameState !== "WORD_SELECTION") {
            return { ok: false, error: "Şu anda kelime belirleme aşamasında değilsiniz." };
        }
        const rateCheck = this.checkActionRateLimit(user?.id, 'set_word', 500);
        if (!rateCheck.ok) return rateCheck;

        if (!user.targetId || user.hasSubmittedWord) {
            return { ok: false, error: "Zaten kelime gönderdiniz veya hedef oyuncunuz yok." };
        }
        if (typeof word !== "string") {
            return { ok: false, error: "Kelime metin formatında olmalıdır." };
        }
        const cleanWord = word.trim().substring(0, 30);
        if (cleanWord.length < 3 || cleanWord.length > 30) {
            return { ok: false, error: "Kelime 3 ile 30 karakter arasında olmalıdır." };
        }
        if (!/^[a-zA-ZçğıöşüÇĞİÖŞÜ0-9\s]+$/.test(cleanWord)) {
            return { ok: false, error: "Kelime yalnızca harf, rakam ve boşluk içerebilir." };
        }

        return { ok: true, data: cleanWord };
    },

   
    validateEditWord(user, room) {
        const activeCheck = this.validateActivePlayer(user, room);
        if (!activeCheck.ok) return activeCheck;

        if (room.gameState !== "WORD_SELECTION") {
            return { ok: false, error: "Şu anda kelime düzenleme aşamasında değilsiniz." };
        }
        if (!user.hasSubmittedWord) {
            return { ok: false, error: "Henüz bir kelime göndermediniz." };
        }
        return { ok: true };
    },

    validateShuffleTargets(user, room) {
        if (!user || !user.isHost) {
            return { ok: false, error: "Yalnızca oda kurucusu hedefleri karıştırabilir." };
        }
        if (!room || room.gameState !== "WORD_SELECTION") {
            return { ok: false, error: "Hedefler yalnızca kelime seçimi aşamasında karıştırılabilir." };
        }
        return { ok: true };
    },

    validatePlaceBet(targetId, user, room) {
        const activeCheck = this.validateActivePlayer(user, room);
        if (!activeCheck.ok) return activeCheck;

        if (room.gameState !== "BETTING") {
            return { ok: false, error: "Şu anda bahis aşamasında değilsiniz." };
        }
        const rateCheck = this.checkActionRateLimit(user?.id, 'place_bet', 500);
        if (!rateCheck.ok) return rateCheck;

        if (user.hasPlacedBet) {
            return { ok: false, error: "Zaten bahis yatırdınız." };
        }
        if (typeof targetId !== "string" || !targetId.trim()) {
            return { ok: false, error: "Geçersiz hedef oyuncu formatı." };
        }
        const cleanTargetId = targetId.trim();
        if (cleanTargetId === user.id) {
            return { ok: false, error: "Kendinize bahis yatıramazsınız!" };
        }
        const target = room.users.find(u => u.id === cleanTargetId && !u.disconnected && u.status === 'playing');
        if (!target) {
            return { ok: false, error: "Bahis yatırılacak aktif oyuncu bulunamadı." };
        }

        return { ok: true, data: target.id };
    },

    validateChat(message, userId) {
        if (typeof message !== "string") {
            return { ok: false, error: "Mesaj metin formatında olmalıdır." };
        }
        const cleanMsg = message.trim();
        if (cleanMsg.length === 0 || cleanMsg.length > 150) {
            return { ok: false, error: "Mesaj 1 ile 150 karakter arasında olmalıdır." };
        }

        const now = Date.now();
        const timestamps = chatRateLimits.get(userId) || [];
        const recent = timestamps.filter(t => now - t < 3000);
        if (recent.length >= 5) {
            return { ok: false, error: "Çok hızlı mesaj gönderiyorsunuz, lütfen biraz bekleyin!" };
        }
        recent.push(now);
        chatRateLimits.set(userId, recent);

        return { ok: true, data: cleanMsg };
    },

    validateAskQuestion(question, user, room) {
        const turnCheck = this.validateCurrentTurn(user, room, "soru sormak");
        if (!turnCheck.ok) return turnCheck;

        const rateCheck = this.checkActionRateLimit(user?.id, 'ask_question', 1000);
        if (!rateCheck.ok) return rateCheck;

        if (room.activeQuestion) {
            return { ok: false, error: "Şu anda devam eden aktif bir soru var!" };
        }
        if (user.russianRouletteActive) {
            return { ok: false, error: "Rus Ruletindesiniz, soru soramazsınız! Yalnızca tahmin yapabilirsiniz." };
        }
        if (user.questionsAskedThisTurn >= 1 && (user.extraQuestions || 0) <= 0) {
            return { ok: false, error: "Bu turdaki soru sorma hakkınızı doldurdunuz!" };
        }

        if (typeof question !== "string") {
            return { ok: false, error: "Soru metin formatında olmalıdır." };
        }
        const cleanQ = question.trim();
        if (cleanQ.length < 3 || cleanQ.length > 150) {
            return { ok: false, error: "Soru en az 3, en fazla 150 karakter olmalıdır." };
        }

        return { ok: true, data: cleanQ };
    },

    validateSubmitVote(vote, user, room) {
        const activeCheck = this.validateActivePlayer(user, room);
        if (!activeCheck.ok) return activeCheck;

        if (room.gameState !== "PLAYING" || !room.activeQuestion) {
            return { ok: false, error: "Şu anda oylanacak aktif bir soru bulunmuyor." };
        }
        const rateCheck = this.checkActionRateLimit(user?.id, 'submit_vote', 300);
        if (!rateCheck.ok) return rateCheck;

        if (room.activeQuestion.askerId === user.id) {
            return { ok: false, error: "Kendi sorduğunuz soruya oy veremezsiniz!" };
        }

        if (typeof vote !== "string") {
            return { ok: false, error: "Oy formatı metin olmalıdır." };
        }
        const normalized = vote.trim().toLowerCase();
        let validVote = "";
        if (normalized === "yes" || normalized === "evet") validVote = "yes";
        else if (normalized === "no" || normalized === "hayır") validVote = "no";
        else if (normalized === "sometimes" || normalized === "bazen" || normalized === "dunno" || normalized === "bilmiyorum") validVote = "sometimes";
        else {
            return { ok: false, error: "Geçersiz oy seçeneği! (Evet, Hayır veya Bazen olmalıdır)" };
        }

        return { ok: true, data: validVote };
    },

    validateGuess(guess, user, room) {
        const turnCheck = this.validateCurrentTurn(user, room, "tahmin yapmak");
        if (!turnCheck.ok) return turnCheck;

        const rateCheck = this.checkActionRateLimit(user?.id, 'guess', 500);
        if (!rateCheck.ok) return rateCheck;

        if (typeof guess !== "string") {
            return { ok: false, error: "Tahmin metin formatında olmalıdır." };
        }
        const cleanGuess = guess.trim();
        if (cleanGuess.length < 3 || cleanGuess.length > 50) {
            return { ok: false, error: "Tahmin en az 3, en fazla 50 karakter olmalıdır." };
        }
        if (!/^[a-zA-ZçğıöşüÇĞİÖŞÜ0-9\s]+$/.test(cleanGuess)) {
            return { ok: false, error: "Tahmin yalnızca harf, rakam ve boşluk içerebilir." };
        }

        return { ok: true, data: cleanGuess };
    },

    validateSkipTurn(user, room) {
        const turnCheck = this.validateCurrentTurn(user, room, "turu geçmek");
        if (!turnCheck.ok) return turnCheck;

        const rateCheck = this.checkActionRateLimit(user?.id, 'skip_turn', 500);
        if (!rateCheck.ok) return rateCheck;

        if (user.russianRouletteActive) {
            return { ok: false, error: "Rus Ruletindeyken turu geçemezsiniz, tahmin etmek zorundasınız!" };
        }
        if (room.activeQuestion) {
            return { ok: false, error: "Aktif soru oylanırken turu geçemezsiniz!" };
        }

        return { ok: true };
    },

    validateSelectJoker(jokerId, user, room) {
        const activeCheck = this.validateActivePlayer(user, room);
        if (!activeCheck.ok) return activeCheck;

        if (room.gameState !== "JOKER_DRAFT") {
            return { ok: false, error: "Şu anda joker seçim aşamasında değilsiniz." };
        }
        const rateCheck = this.checkActionRateLimit(user?.id, 'select_joker', 500);
        if (!rateCheck.ok) return rateCheck;

        if (user.hasDrafted) {
            return { ok: false, error: "Zaten bir joker seçtiniz." };
        }
        const idNum = Number(jokerId);
        if (!Number.isInteger(idNum) || !user.jokerChoices || !user.jokerChoices.includes(idNum)) {
            return { ok: false, error: "Seçilen joker size sunulan seçenekler arasında yok!" };
        }

        return { ok: true, data: idNum };
    },

    validateUseJoker(payload, user, room) {
        const turnCheck = this.validateCurrentTurn(user, room, "joker kullanmak");
        if (!turnCheck.ok) return turnCheck;

        const rateCheck = this.checkActionRateLimit(user?.id, 'use_joker', 800);
        if (!rateCheck.ok) return rateCheck;

        if (user.russianRouletteActive) {
            return { ok: false, error: "Rus Ruletindeyken joker kullanamazsınız!" };
        }
        if (room.roundCount < 3) {
            return { ok: false, error: "Jokerler 3. turdan itibaren kullanılabilir!" };
        }
        if (user.joker === null || user.joker === undefined) {
            return { ok: false, error: "Kullanabileceğiniz bir jokeriniz yok!" };
        }
        if (user.hasUsedJoker) {
            return { ok: false, error: "Joker hakkınızı bu oyunda zaten kullandınız!" };
        }
        if (!payload || typeof payload !== "object") {
            return { ok: false, error: "Geçersiz joker parametresi." };
        }

        const cleanPayload = {};
        if (payload.targetId !== undefined && payload.targetId !== null) {
            if (typeof payload.targetId !== "string" || !payload.targetId.trim()) {
                return { ok: false, error: "Geçersiz hedef oyuncu formatı." };
            }
            const cleanTargetId = payload.targetId.trim();
            const target = room.users.find(u => u.id === cleanTargetId && !u.disconnected && u.status === 'playing');
            if (!target) {
                return { ok: false, error: "Hedef oyuncu bulunamadı veya artık oyunda değil." };
            }
            cleanPayload.targetId = target.id;
        }

        if (payload.newWord !== undefined && payload.newWord !== null) {
            if (typeof payload.newWord !== "string") {
                return { ok: false, error: "Yeni kelime metin formatında olmalıdır." };
            }
            const cleanWord = payload.newWord.trim();
            if (cleanWord.length === 0 || cleanWord.length > 30) {
                return { ok: false, error: "Kelime 1 ile 30 karakter arasında olmalıdır." };
            }
            cleanPayload.newWord = cleanWord;
        }

        return { ok: true, data: cleanPayload };
    },
    validateStartObjection(user, room) {
        const activeCheck = this.validateActivePlayer(user, room);
        if (!activeCheck.ok) return activeCheck;

        if (room.gameState !== "PLAYING") {
            return { ok: false, error: "Şike itirazı yalnızca oyun oynanırken yapılabilir." };
        }
        const rateCheck = this.checkActionRateLimit(user?.id, 'start_objection', 1000);
        if (!rateCheck.ok) return rateCheck;

        if (user.russianRouletteActive) {
            return { ok: false, error: "Rus Ruletindeyken şike itirazı yapamazsınız!" };
        }
        if (room.objection) {
            return { ok: false, error: "Şu anda zaten devam eden bir şike itirazı var!" };
        }
        if (room.hasObjectionUsed) {
            return { ok: false, error: "Bu oyunda şike itirazı hakkı zaten kullanıldı!" };
        }

        return { ok: true };
    },

    validateVoteObjection(vote, user, room) {
        const activeCheck = this.validateActivePlayer(user, room);
        if (!activeCheck.ok) return activeCheck;

        if (room.gameState !== "PLAYING" || !room.objection) {
            return { ok: false, error: "Şu anda aktif bir itiraz oylaması yok." };
        }
        const rateCheck = this.checkActionRateLimit(user?.id, 'vote_objection', 300);
        if (!rateCheck.ok) return rateCheck;

        if (typeof vote !== "boolean") {
            return { ok: false, error: "İtiraz oyu doğru/yanlış (boolean) formatında olmalıdır." };
        }

        return { ok: true, data: vote };
    },

    validateHeadRotation(payload, userId) {
        if (!payload || typeof payload !== "object") return { ok: false };
        const pitch = Number(payload.pitch);
        const yaw = Number(payload.yaw);
        if (!Number.isFinite(pitch) || !Number.isFinite(yaw)) return { ok: false };
        if (pitch < -3.5 || pitch > 3.5 || yaw < -3.5 || yaw > 3.5) return { ok: false };

        const now = Date.now();
        const tracker = headRotRateLimits.get(userId) || { count: 0, windowStart: now };
        if (now - tracker.windowStart > 1000) {
            tracker.count = 1;
            tracker.windowStart = now;
        } else {
            tracker.count++;
            if (tracker.count > 30) return { ok: false }; // Flood durumunda sessizce yut
        }
        headRotRateLimits.set(userId, tracker);

        return { ok: true, data: { pitch, yaw } };
    },

    validateTaunt(type, userId) {
        if (typeof type !== "string" || type.trim().length === 0 || type.length > 30) {
            return { ok: false, error: "Geçersiz taunt tipi." };
        }

        const now = Date.now();
        const lastTime = tauntRateLimits.get(userId) || 0;
        if (now - lastTime < 2000) {
            return { ok: false, error: "Taunt için lütfen 2 saniye bekleyin!" };
        }
        tauntRateLimits.set(userId, now);

        return { ok: true, data: type.trim() };
    }
};

module.exports = payloadValidator;

const mongoose = require("mongoose");

const userSchema = new mongoose.Schema({
    username: {
        type: String,
        required: true,
        unique: true,
        trim: true,
    },
    email: {
        type: String,
        required: true,
        unique: true,
        trim: true,
        lowercase: true,
    },
    password: {
        type: String,
        required: true,
    },
    avatar: {
        type: String,
        default: 'Warrior'
    },
    gold: {
        type: Number,
        default: 0
    },
    unlockedAvatars: {
        type: [String],
        default: ['Warrior']
    },
    pedestal: {
        type: String,
        default: 'default_stone'
    },
    unlockedPedestals: {
        type: [String],
        default: ['default_stone']
    },
    nameColor: {
        type: String,
        default: 'text-white'
    },
    unlockedNameColors: {
        type: [String],
        default: ['text-white']
    },
    title: {
        type: String,
        default: ''
    },
    unlockedTitles: {
        type: [String],
        default: []
    },
    lastLoginDate: {
        type: Date,
        default: null
    },
    loginStreak: {
        type: Number,
        default: 0
    },

    reputation: {
        type: Number,
        default: 0
    },
    quests: {
        active: [{
            id: String,
            type: { type: String },
            target: Number,
            progress: { type: Number, default: 0 },
            rewardGold: { type: Number, default: 0 },
            rewardRep: { type: Number, default: 0 },
            title: String,
            isClaimed: { type: Boolean, default: false }
        }],
        lastResetDate: { type: String, default: null }
    },
    createdAt: {
        type: Date,
        default: Date.now
    }
});

module.exports = mongoose.model("User", userSchema);

const express = require("express");
const router = express.Router();
const User = require("../models/User");
const bcrypt = require("bcryptjs");
const jwt = require("jsonwebtoken");

const JWT_SECRET = process.env.JWT_SECRET;
if (!JWT_SECRET) {
    console.error("FATAL ERROR: JWT_SECRET ortam değişkeni tanımlanmamış!");
    process.exit(1);
}

const authMiddleware = (req, res, next) => {
    const token = req.header("Authorization")?.replace("Bearer ", "");
    if (!token) return res.status(401).json({ error: "Erişim reddedildi. Token yok." });

    try {
        const decoded = jwt.verify(token, JWT_SECRET);
        req.user = decoded;
        next();
    } catch (err) {
        res.status(401).json({ error: "Geçersiz veya süresi dolmuş token." });
    }
};

function getRankInfo(reputation) {
    let title = "Çaylak";
    let nextThreshold = 400;
    
    if (reputation >= 5000) {
        title = "Kimbuki Üstadı";
        nextThreshold = null;
    } else if (reputation >= 2500) {
        title = "Zihin Okuyucu";
        nextThreshold = 5000;
    } else if (reputation >= 1000) {
        title = "Dedektif";
        nextThreshold = 2500;
    } else if (reputation >= 400) {
        title = "Gözlemci";
        nextThreshold = 1000;
    }
    
    return { title, reputation, nextThreshold };
}

function generateDailyQuests() {
    const shuffled = [...QUEST_POOL].sort(() => 0.5 - Math.random());
    return shuffled.slice(0, 3).map(q => ({ ...q, progress: 0, isClaimed: false }));
}

router.post("/register", async (req, res) => {
    try {
        const { username, email, password } = req.body;
        
        if (typeof username !== 'string' || typeof email !== 'string' || typeof password !== 'string') {
            return res.status(400).json({ error: "Geçersiz veri formatı." });
        }

        if (!username || !email || !password) {
            return res.status(400).json({ error: "Lütfen kullanıcı adı, e-posta ve şifre girin." });
        }
        
        const emailRegex = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/;
        if (!emailRegex.test(email)) {
            return res.status(400).json({ error: "Lütfen geçerli bir e-posta adresi girin." });
        }

        if (username.length < 3) {
            return res.status(400).json({ error: "Kullanıcı adı en az 3 karakter olmalıdır." });
        }

        if (password.length < 8) {
            return res.status(400).json({ error: "Şifre en az 8 karakter olmalıdır." });
        }
        if (!/(?=.*[a-z])/.test(password)) {
            return res.status(400).json({ error: "Şifre en az bir küçük harf içermelidir." });
        }
        if (!/(?=.*[A-Z])/.test(password)) {
            return res.status(400).json({ error: "Şifre en az bir büyük harf içermelidir." });
        }
        if (!/(?=.*\d)/.test(password)) {
            return res.status(400).json({ error: "Şifre en az bir rakam (sayı) içermelidir." });
        }

        const existingUser = await User.findOne({ $or: [{ username }, { email }] });
        if (existingUser) {
            return res.status(400).json({ error: "Bu kullanıcı adı veya e-posta zaten kullanımda." });
        }

        const salt = await bcrypt.genSalt(10);
        const hashedPassword = await bcrypt.hash(password, salt);

        const user = new User({
            username,
            email,
            password: hashedPassword,
            avatar: "Warrior",
            pedestal: "default_stone",
            gold: 0,
            unlockedAvatars: ["Warrior"],
            unlockedPedestals: ["default_stone"],
            unlockedNameColors: ["text-white"],
            unlockedTitles: []
        });
        await user.save();

        const token = jwt.sign({ id: user._id }, JWT_SECRET, { expiresIn: "30d" });

        res.status(201).json({
            token,
            user: { 
                id: user._id, 
                username: user.username, 
                email: user.email, 
                avatar: user.avatar,
                gold: user.gold,
                unlockedAvatars: user.unlockedAvatars,
                rankInfo: getRankInfo(user.reputation || 0)
            }
        });
    } catch (error) {
        console.error("Register error:", error);
        res.status(500).json({ error: "Sunucu hatası" });
    }
});

router.post("/login", async (req, res) => {
    try {
        const { email, password } = req.body;
        
        if (typeof email !== 'string' || typeof password !== 'string') {
            return res.status(400).json({ error: "Geçersiz veri formatı." });
        }

        if (!email || !password) {
            return res.status(400).json({ error: "Lütfen e-posta ve şifre girin." });
        }

        const user = await User.findOne({ email });
        if (!user) {
            return res.status(400).json({ error: "Kullanıcı bulunamadı." });
        }

        const isMatch = await bcrypt.compare(password, user.password);
        if (!isMatch) {
            return res.status(400).json({ error: "Hatalı şifre." });
        }

        let isModified = false;
        if (!user.unlockedAvatars) user.unlockedAvatars = [];
        if (!user.unlockedAvatars.includes('Warrior')) {
            user.unlockedAvatars.push('Warrior');
            isModified = true;
        }
        if (user.avatar && user.avatar.startsWith('default-')) {
            user.avatar = 'Warrior';
            isModified = true;
        }
        if (user.unlockedAvatars.some(a => a.startsWith('default-'))) {
            user.unlockedAvatars = user.unlockedAvatars.filter(a => !a.startsWith('default-'));
            isModified = true;
        }
        if (!user.unlockedPedestals) user.unlockedPedestals = [];
        if (!user.unlockedPedestals.includes('default_stone')) {
            user.unlockedPedestals.push('default_stone');
            if (!user.pedestal) user.pedestal = 'default_stone';
            isModified = true;
        }
        if (!user.unlockedNameColors) user.unlockedNameColors = [];
        if (!user.unlockedNameColors.includes('text-white')) {
            user.unlockedNameColors.push('text-white');
            if (!user.nameColor) user.nameColor = 'text-white';
            isModified = true;
        }
        if (!user.unlockedTitles) {
            user.unlockedTitles = [];
            isModified = true;
        }
        if (isModified) await user.save();

        const token = jwt.sign({ id: user._id }, JWT_SECRET, { expiresIn: "30d" });

        res.json({
            token,
            user: { 
                id: user._id, 
                username: user.username, 
                email: user.email, 
                avatar: user.avatar,
                pedestal: user.pedestal,
                nameColor: user.nameColor,
                title: user.title,
                gold: user.gold,
                unlockedAvatars: user.unlockedAvatars,
                unlockedPedestals: user.unlockedPedestals,
                unlockedNameColors: user.unlockedNameColors,
                unlockedTitles: user.unlockedTitles,
                rankInfo: getRankInfo(user.reputation || 0)
            }
        });
    } catch (error) {
        console.error("Login error:", error);
        res.status(500).json({ error: "Sunucu hatası" });
    }
});

router.get("/me", authMiddleware, async (req, res) => {
    try {
        const user = await User.findById(req.user.id).select("-password");
        if (!user) {
            return res.status(404).json({ error: "Kullanıcı bulunamadı." });
        }

        let arraysUpdated = false;

        if (!user.unlockedAvatars) user.unlockedAvatars = [];
        if (!user.unlockedAvatars.includes('Warrior')) {
            user.unlockedAvatars.push('Warrior');
            arraysUpdated = true;
        }
        if (user.avatar && user.avatar.startsWith('default-')) {
            user.avatar = 'Warrior';
            arraysUpdated = true;
        }
        if (user.unlockedAvatars.some(a => a.startsWith('default-'))) {
            user.unlockedAvatars = user.unlockedAvatars.filter(a => !a.startsWith('default-'));
            arraysUpdated = true;
        }
        if (!user.unlockedPedestals) user.unlockedPedestals = [];
        if (!user.unlockedPedestals.includes('default_stone')) {
            user.unlockedPedestals.push('default_stone');
            if (!user.pedestal) user.pedestal = 'default_stone';
            arraysUpdated = true;
        }

        let canClaimDaily = false;
        let streak = user.loginStreak || 0;
        const now = new Date();
        const today = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate());

        if (!user.lastLoginDate || user.lastLoginDate < today) {
            canClaimDaily = true;
            const yesterday = new Date(today);
            yesterday.setDate(yesterday.getDate() - 1);
            
            if (!user.lastLoginDate || user.lastLoginDate < yesterday) {
                streak = 0; 
            }
        }

        if (!user.unlockedNameColors) user.unlockedNameColors = [];
        if (!user.unlockedNameColors.includes('text-white')) {
            user.unlockedNameColors.push('text-white');
            if (!user.nameColor) user.nameColor = 'text-white';
            arraysUpdated = true;
        }

        if (!user.unlockedTitles) {
            user.unlockedTitles = [];
            arraysUpdated = true;
        }

        const todayStr = new Date().toDateString();
        let questsUpdated = false;
        
        if (!user.quests) {
            user.quests = {};
        }

        if (user.quests.lastResetDate !== todayStr) {
            user.quests.active = generateDailyQuests();
            user.quests.lastResetDate = todayStr;
            user.markModified('quests');
            questsUpdated = true;
        }

        if (arraysUpdated) {
            user.markModified('unlockedAvatars');
            user.markModified('unlockedPedestals');
            user.markModified('unlockedNameColors');
            user.markModified('unlockedTitles');
        }

        if (questsUpdated || canClaimDaily || arraysUpdated) await user.save();

        res.json({
            id: user._id,
            username: user.username,
            email: user.email,
            avatar: user.avatar,
            pedestal: user.pedestal,
            nameColor: user.nameColor,
            title: user.title,
            gold: user.gold,
            unlockedAvatars: user.unlockedAvatars,
            unlockedPedestals: user.unlockedPedestals,
            unlockedNameColors: user.unlockedNameColors,
            unlockedTitles: user.unlockedTitles,
            rankInfo: getRankInfo(user.reputation || 0),
            quests: user.quests?.active || [],
            canClaimDaily,
            loginStreak: streak
        });
    } catch (error) {
        console.error("GET /me error:", error);
        res.status(500).json({ error: "Sunucu hatası" });
    }
});

router.post("/daily-reward", authMiddleware, async (req, res) => {
    try {
        const user = await User.findById(req.user.id);
        if (!user) return res.status(404).json({ error: "Kullanıcı bulunamadı." });

        const now = new Date();
        const today = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate());

        if (user.lastLoginDate && user.lastLoginDate >= today) {
            return res.status(400).json({ error: "Bugünün ödülünü zaten aldın!" });
        }

        let streak = user.loginStreak || 0;
        const yesterday = new Date(today);
        yesterday.setDate(yesterday.getDate() - 1);

        if (user.lastLoginDate && user.lastLoginDate >= yesterday) {
            streak++;
        } else {
            streak = 1;
        }
        if (streak > 7) streak = 7;

        const rewardAmounts = { 1: 50, 2: 100, 3: 150, 4: 200, 5: 250, 6: 300, 7: 500 };
        const amount = rewardAmounts[streak] || 50;

        user.gold += amount;
        user.lastLoginDate = today;
        user.loginStreak = streak;
        await user.save();

        res.json({ success: true, amount, streak, gold: user.gold });
    } catch (error) {
        res.status(500).json({ error: "Sunucu hatası" });
    }
});

router.put("/profile", authMiddleware, async (req, res) => {
    try {
        const { username, avatar, pedestal, nameColor, title } = req.body;
        
        if (username && typeof username !== 'string') return res.status(400).json({ error: "Geçersiz format" });

        const user = await User.findById(req.user.id);
        
        if (!user) return res.status(404).json({ error: "Kullanıcı bulunamadı." });

        if (username && username !== user.username) {
            const existing = await User.findOne({ username });
            if (existing) return res.status(400).json({ error: "Bu kullanıcı adı zaten kullanımda." });
            user.username = username;
        }

        if (!user.unlockedAvatars) user.unlockedAvatars = ['Warrior'];
        if (!user.unlockedPedestals) user.unlockedPedestals = ['default_stone'];
        if (!user.unlockedNameColors) user.unlockedNameColors = ['text-white'];
        if (!user.unlockedTitles) user.unlockedTitles = [];

        if (avatar && typeof avatar === 'string' && user.unlockedAvatars.includes(avatar)) {
            user.avatar = avatar;
        }

        if (pedestal && typeof pedestal === 'string' && user.unlockedPedestals.includes(pedestal)) {
            user.pedestal = pedestal;
        }

        if (nameColor && typeof nameColor === 'string' && user.unlockedNameColors.includes(nameColor)) {
            user.nameColor = nameColor;
        }

        if (title !== undefined && typeof title === 'string' && (title === '' || user.unlockedTitles.includes(title))) {
            user.title = title;
        }

        await user.save();
        res.json({ 
            id: user._id, 
            username: user.username, 
            avatar: user.avatar,
            pedestal: user.pedestal,
            nameColor: user.nameColor,
            title: user.title,
            gold: user.gold,
            unlockedAvatars: user.unlockedAvatars,
            unlockedPedestals: user.unlockedPedestals,
            unlockedNameColors: user.unlockedNameColors,
            unlockedTitles: user.unlockedTitles,
            rankInfo: getRankInfo(user.reputation || 0)
        });
    } catch (error) {
        res.status(500).json({ error: "Sunucu hatası" });
    }
});

router.post("/claim-quest", authMiddleware, async (req, res) => {
    try {
        const { questId } = req.body;
        if (typeof questId !== 'string') return res.status(400).json({ error: "Geçersiz format" });

        const user = await User.findById(req.user.id);
        
        if (!user || !user.quests || !user.quests.active) return res.status(400).json({ error: "Görev bulunamadı." });
        
        const quest = user.quests.active.find(q => q.id === questId);
        if (!quest) return res.status(400).json({ error: "Görev bulunamadı." });
        if (quest.isClaimed) return res.status(400).json({ error: "Bu ödülü zaten aldın." });
        if (quest.progress < quest.target) return res.status(400).json({ error: "Görev henüz tamamlanmadı." });

        quest.isClaimed = true;
        user.gold += quest.rewardGold;
        user.reputation += quest.rewardRep;
        
        user.markModified('quests');
        await user.save();

        res.json({
            success: true,
            gold: user.gold,
            rankInfo: getRankInfo(user.reputation),
            quests: user.quests.active
        });
    } catch (error) {
        res.status(500).json({ error: "Sunucu hatası" });
    }
});

module.exports = router;

const express = require("express");
const router = express.Router();
const User = require("../models/User");
const jwt = require("jsonwebtoken");

const JWT_SECRET = process.env.JWT_SECRET;
if (!JWT_SECRET) {
    console.error("FATAL ERROR: JWT_SECRET ortam değişkeni tanımlanmamış!");
    process.exit(1);
}

const authMiddleware = (req, res, next) => {
    const token = req.header("Authorization")?.replace("Bearer ", "");
    if (!token) return res.status(401).json({ error: "Erişim reddedildi. Token yok." });

    try {
        const decoded = jwt.verify(token, JWT_SECRET);
        req.user = decoded;
        next();
    } catch (err) {
        res.status(401).json({ error: "Geçersiz veya süresi dolmuş token." });
    }
};

const PREMIUM_AVATARS = [
    { id: 'Warrior', price: 100, label: 'Savaşçı', icon: '⚔️', type: 'avatar' },
    { id: 'Wizard', price: 200, label: 'Büyücü', icon: '🔮', type: 'avatar' },
    { id: 'Rogue', price: 300, label: 'Suikastçi', icon: '🗡️', type: 'avatar' },
    { id: 'Ranger', price: 400, label: 'Okçu', icon: '🏹', type: 'avatar' },
    { id: 'Monk', price: 500, label: 'Keşiş', icon: '🥋', type: 'avatar' },
    { id: 'Cleric', price: 600, label: 'Şifacı', icon: '✨', type: 'avatar' }
];

const PREMIUM_PEDESTALS = [
    { id: 'gold_pedestal', price: 500, label: 'Altın Kaide', icon: '🏆', type: 'pedestal' },
    { id: 'lava_ring', price: 800, label: 'Lav Halkası', icon: '🌋', type: 'pedestal' },
    { id: 'ice_block', price: 600, label: 'Buz Kütlesi', icon: '🧊', type: 'pedestal' },
    { id: 'cloud_base', price: 1000, label: 'Uçan Bulut', icon: '☁️', type: 'pedestal' }
];

const PREMIUM_COLORS = [
    { id: 'color_neon_pink', price: 1000, label: 'Neon Pembe', icon: '🎀', type: 'color' },
    { id: 'color_electric_blue', price: 1000, label: 'Elektrik Mavisi', icon: '⚡', type: 'color' },
    { id: 'color_gold', price: 2000, label: 'Saf Altın', icon: '✨', type: 'color' }
];

const PREMIUM_TITLES = [
    { id: 'title_rich', price: 5000, label: 'Ünvan: Zengin', icon: '👑', type: 'title' },
    { id: 'title_troll', price: 1500, label: 'Ünvan: Trol', icon: '🤡', type: 'title' },
    { id: 'title_collector', price: 2500, label: 'Ünvan: Koleksiyoncu', icon: '💎', type: 'title' }
];

const ALL_ITEMS = [...PREMIUM_AVATARS, ...PREMIUM_PEDESTALS, ...PREMIUM_COLORS, ...PREMIUM_TITLES];

router.get("/items", (req, res) => {
    res.json(ALL_ITEMS);
});

router.post("/buy", authMiddleware, async (req, res) => {
    try {
        const { itemId } = req.body;
        const itemToBuy = ALL_ITEMS.find(a => a.id === itemId);
        
        if (!itemToBuy) {
            return res.status(400).json({ error: "Böyle bir eşya bulunamadı." });
        }

        let collectionKey = 'unlockedAvatars';
        if (itemToBuy.type === 'pedestal') collectionKey = 'unlockedPedestals';
        else if (itemToBuy.type === 'color') collectionKey = 'unlockedNameColors';
        else if (itemToBuy.type === 'title') collectionKey = 'unlockedTitles';

        const updatedUser = await User.findOneAndUpdate(
            {
                _id: req.user.id,
                gold: { $gte: itemToBuy.price },
                [collectionKey]: { $ne: itemId }
            },
            {
                $inc: { gold: -itemToBuy.price },
                $addToSet: { [collectionKey]: itemId }
            },
            { new: true }
        );

        if (!updatedUser) {
            const existingUser = await User.findById(req.user.id);
            if (!existingUser) {
                return res.status(404).json({ error: "Kullanıcı bulunamadı." });
            }
            if (existingUser[collectionKey]?.includes(itemId)) {
                return res.status(400).json({ error: "Bu eşyaya zaten sahipsiniz." });
            }
            if (existingUser.gold < itemToBuy.price) {
                return res.status(400).json({ error: "Yeterli altınınız yok." });
            }
            return res.status(400).json({ error: "Satın alma işlemi gerçekleştirilemedi." });
        }

        if (itemToBuy.type === 'avatar' && updatedUser.quests && updatedUser.quests.active) {
            let questUpdated = false;
            updatedUser.quests.active.forEach(q => {
                if (q.type === 'buy_avatar' && !q.isClaimed && q.progress < q.target) {
                    q.progress++;
                    questUpdated = true;
                }
            });
            if (questUpdated) {
                updatedUser.markModified('quests');
                await updatedUser.save();
            }
        }

        res.json({
            message: "Satın alma başarılı!",
            gold: updatedUser.gold,
            unlockedAvatars: updatedUser.unlockedAvatars,
            unlockedPedestals: updatedUser.unlockedPedestals,
            unlockedNameColors: updatedUser.unlockedNameColors,
            unlockedTitles: updatedUser.unlockedTitles
        });
    } catch (error) {
        console.error("Shop buy error:", error);
        res.status(500).json({ error: "Sunucu hatası" });
    }
});

module.exports = router;

const express = require("express");
const router = express.Router();
const User = require("../models/User");

router.get("/live", (req, res) => {
    const gameManager = req.app.locals.gameManager;
    if (!gameManager) return res.json({ activeRooms: 0, activePlayers: 0 });
    res.json(gameManager.getStats());
});

router.get("/leaderboard", async (req, res) => {
    try {
        const topUsers = await User.find({}, "username gold reputation avatar nameColor title").sort({ reputation: -1, gold: -1 }).limit(10).lean();
        res.json(topUsers);
    } catch (err) {
        res.status(500).json({ error: "Sunucu hatasi" });
    }
});

module.exports = router;
